### [1] [RTL Fault Injection of a Deployed Graph Neural Network Trigger for Belle II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03153)
*Georgios Sotiropoulos,Marc Neu,Tanja Harbaum,Torben Ferber,Jürgen Becker*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文首次在寄存器传输级对Belle II实验中部署的GNN-ETM硬件神经网络一级触发器开展单粒子翻转（SEU）故障注入研究，系统评估其死锁、超时与数据包完整性失效三类关键故障模式，揭示现有监控架构存在不对称性，并提出基于级间活性监测的新方法，显著提升MTTF估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型高能物理实验中，部署于FPGA的一级触发系统因靠近相互作用点而遭受强辐射，导致SEU引发死锁、超时或数据包损坏，威胁实时触发可靠性及科学产出。

Method: 在RTL层面针对GNN-ETM触发器开展大规模SEU故障注入：覆盖211,245个信号，注入1,442,840次翻转；设计双轨验证方案，对比输出端监控与新增的级间活性监控效能；量化不同故障模式的发生率与传播路径。

Result: 发现现有输出端监控存在严重监测盲区，导致MTTF估计偏差高达78.7%；级间活性监控可精准定位故障传播链；识别出触发流水线中若干高优先级加固节点。

Conclusion: RTL级故障注入是评估硬件神经网络触发器辐射鲁棒性的有效手段；级间活性监控相较传统输出监控显著提升故障检测完备性与MTTF建模准确性；结果为未来抗辐射触发器架构设计与加固策略提供定量依据。

Abstract: As particle physics detectors grow in scale, High Energy Physics experiments must process ever-increasing data volumes. Level-1 trigger systems, implemented on Field-Programmable Gate Arrays and increasingly using neural-network algorithms, filter this data in real time. However, their proximity to the interaction point exposes them to radiation, which can corrupt outputs, stall processing pipelines, or damage hardware, with significant financial and scientific consequences. In this work, we present the first Register Transfer Level fault-injection study of a deployed Level-1 hardware neural-network trigger, GNN-ETM in the Belle II trigger system. We target three failure modes most consequential to a real-time trigger pipeline: deadlocks, timeouts, and packet-integrity violations. Through two complementary campaigns, we inject 1 442 840 Single-Event Upsets across 211 245 signals. We find a monitoring asymmetry in the existing verification infrastructure and propose inter-stage liveness monitoring as a more accurate alternative to output-only observation, showing that Mean Time To Failure estimates from the two approaches differ by up to 78.7%. The resulting per-stage data identifies the highest-priority hardening targets.

</details>


### [2] [Searching for Dark Photons with a room-temperature dielectric haloscope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03240)
*Siyin Li,Paschos Ioannis,Zhengyin Yang,Alexandros Spiliotis,Husheng Guan,Pavlos G. Savvidis,Shengchao Li*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用室温介电多层腔（dielectric multilayer haloscope）搜寻质量为1.9 eV/c²的暗光子暗物质，通过增强2 eV附近光子转换并结合高灵敏CMOS焦平面探测，未观测到信号，给出耦合强度κ < 4.0×10⁻¹³（90% CL）的上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗光子作为轻质量暗物质候选者，其与标准模型光子的kinetic mixing（参数κ）在eV能标尚未被充分探测；需发展高灵敏、窄带、室温运行的新型探测技术。

Method: 采用介电多层堆栈构成谐振增强型haloscope，在~2 eV（对应1.9 eV/c²暗光子质量）实现光子-暗光子振荡共振增强；利用空间分辨CMOS焦平面实现单光子级探测；通过原位标定stack-lens-CMOS系统响应函数，并基于标定后的焦平面图像模板进行似然推断。

Result: 在904小时信号搜索数据与404小时背景控制数据下，未发现显著超出，对质量m_A' = 1.9 eV/c²的暗光子暗物质，得到κ < 4.0×10⁻¹³（90%置信水平）的直接实验上限。

Conclusion: 该工作首次将介电多层haloscope与CMOS单光子成像结合，实现了eV量级暗光子暗物质的前沿灵敏度探测，为未来宽谱扫描和更高灵敏度实验提供了可扩展技术路径。

Abstract: We present a search for dark-photon dark matter with a room-temperature dielectric multilayer haloscope. The dielectric stack enhances photon conversion near 2 eV, and a spatially resolved CMOS focal plane records the emitted photons with few-photon sensitivity. We calibrate the stack-lens-CMOS response in situ and use the calibrated focal-plane pattern in a template-based inference. With 904 h of search data and 404 h of background-control data, we observe no excess and set a 90% confidence-level upper limit of $κ< 4.0\times10^{-13}$ for dark-photon dark matter with mass 1.9$\,\mathrm{eV}/c^2$.

</details>


### [3] [Flow-Based Surrogates for High-Dimensional Likelihoods in Experimental Neutrino Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03477)
*Mathias El Baz,Lorenzo Giannessi,Adrien Blanchet,Federico Sánchez*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出使用归一化流（normalizing flows）建模高维非高斯近探测器似然函数，以提升长基线中微子振荡实验中系统不确定性的约束精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长基线中微子实验需利用近探测器数据约束未振荡中微子通量的系统不确定性，但当似然函数高维且非高斯时，传统高斯近似效果差，亟需更精确、可移植的统计建模方法。

Method: 基于初始高斯似然近似，构建耦合变换与自回归样条流（autoregressive spline flows）混合的归一化流架构，并在含110个系统参数（其中10个引入后验非高斯性）的代表性近探测器似然副本上进行训练。

Result: 训练模型达到98%的相对有效样本量（ESS），远高于高斯近似的约5%；能精确复现MCMC参考结果，同时保持闭式表达、可采样及逐点可评估特性。

Conclusion: 归一化流为高维非高斯似然建模提供了高保真、便携、实用的解决方案，适用于下游不确定性传播及未来近-远探测器联合拟合。

Abstract: Precision long-baseline neutrino experiments use near-detector data to constrain systematic uncertainties on the unoscillated neutrino flux, a prerequisite for accurate oscillation parameter measurements at the far detector. When the constrained likelihood is high-dimensional and non-Gaussian, this procedure demands advanced statistical treatment. Here we show that normalizing flows provide faithful and portable likelihood models for this problem. Leveraging an initial Gaussian approximation of the likelihood, we train a hybrid architecture combining coupling transformations and autoregressive spline flows. We demonstrate the method on a representative near-detector likelihood replica with 110 systematic uncertainty parameters, 10 of which explicitly introduce non-Gaussianities in the posterior. The trained model achieves a relative effective sample size of 98%, compared with about 5% for the Gaussian approximation, and reproduces a Markov chain Monte Carlo reference while remaining closed-form, samplable, and pointwise evaluable, making it suited to downstream uncertainty propagation and future near-detector to far-detector fits.

</details>


### [4] [GPU-Accelerated Matrix-Based Hough Transform for Online Track Reconstruction in the STCF MDC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04067)
*Liang Peng,Baolin Zhang,Zhaoli Guo,Jiarui Zhao,Yizhen Ma,Aonan Zhu,Huilin Li,Zhujun Fang*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向STCF主漂移室（MDC）实时轨迹重建的GPU加速矩阵化Hough变换方法，通过算法-架构协同设计，将共形Hough变换重构为规则矩阵运算，适配CUDA线程与内存层次结构，在保持93.04%信号保留率的同时，将命中点体积压缩至34.92%，处理1000事件仅需0.14秒，较CPU基准提速151.57倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: STCF高亮度运行导致MDC中低横动量粒子轨迹弯曲强、多圈、高占空比与高背景，对实时轨迹重建提出严峻挑战，亟需高效并行化算法以满足在线处理带宽与精度双重约束。

Method: 基于共形Hough变换，采用算法-架构协同设计：将不规则参数空间计算重表述为规则矩阵运算；依据GPU线程块/ warp结构组织计算流；优化全局/共享/寄存器内存访问模式以最小化数据搬移与延迟；实现端到端GPU原生轨迹重建流水线。

Result: 在五类典型模拟物理道（含标称本底）下，平均信号保留率达93.04%，命中点压缩比为34.92%；单GPU处理1000事件耗时0.14 s，相较CPU基准实现151.57×加速；验证了该方法在吞吐量与精度间的最优权衡。

Conclusion: 所提GPU加速矩阵化Hough变换方法显著提升了STCF MDC在线轨迹重建的实时性与效率，为未来高亮度对撞机实验中的实时粒子识别与触发系统提供了可扩展、高保真的新范式。

Abstract: The Super Tau-Charm Facility (STCF) is a proposed next-generation high-luminosity electron-positron collider operating at center-of-mass energies of 2-7 GeV for precision studies of tau-charm physics. Its high event rate, detector occupancy, and background level impose stringent requirements on real-time track reconstruction in MDC, particularly for low-transverse-momentum particles with strongly curved or multi-turn trajectories. To address this challenge, we develop a GPU-accelerated matrix-based Hough transform method for online track reconstruction in the STCF MDC. Following an algorithm-architecture co-design paradigm, the data representation and computational workflow of the conformal Hough transform are reformulated for GPU execution. The original irregular parameter-space computations are organized into regular matrix-based operations, and the core computations are adapted to CUDA thread organization and the GPU memory hierarchy to exploit the inherent parallelism of the Hough transform and reduce computational and data-transfer overhead. Tests on five representative simulated physics channels with nominal background overlay show an average signal retention ratio of 93.04%, while reducing the retained hit volume to 34.92% of the original level. The GPU implementation processes 1,000 events in approximately 0.14s, achieving a speedup of 151.57 x compared with the CPU baseline. These results demonstrate that the proposed method substantially improves track reconstruction throughput while preserving track-associated hits, providing a new methodological perspective for real-time track reconstruction in future high-luminosity particle-collider experiments.

</details>


### [5] [Neural-Network-Assisted Binary Template Construction for Matrix-Based Pattern Matching in the STCF MDC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04271)
*Zhaoli Guo,Qingyu Li,Baolin Zhang,Jiarui Zhao,Aonan Zhu,Liang Peng,Huilin Li*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经网络辅助的二值模板库构建框架，用于超级陶粲工厂主漂移室（MDC）局部径迹段寻找中的矩阵式模式匹配；该方法将模板构造建模为可微多目标优化问题，训练后仅导出二值模板对嵌入现有位运算匹配流程，不引入在线推理开销，兼顾高信噪比、高鲁棒性与确定性实时性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Super Tau-Charm Facility高亮度运行导致高事例率与高本底，传统基于固定模板的位运算模式匹配在MDC局部径迹段寻找中面临检测效率低导致信号丢失、本底高引发误触发等瓶颈，亟需提升模板库的物理性能而不牺牲在线触发的确定性与速度。

Method: 将二值模板库构造建模为带多重物理约束（如效率、假率、动量覆盖）的可微多目标优化问题，采用神经网络联合学习模板参数；训练完成后仅提取二值模板对（±1），部署至现有基于位运算的矩阵匹配流水线，全程无在线神经网络推理。

Result: 仿真结果表明：在有限探测效率下，所生成模板库在不同横动量区间和本底水平下均保持较高信号保留率；模板覆盖范围可灵活调节；且完全兼容原有确定性、低延迟、并行化在线触发架构。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解耦物理性能优化与实时计算约束，通过离线AI驱动模板设计+在线确定性匹配，为高亮度对撞机实验的AI增强型在线数据处理提供了新范式。

Abstract: The Super Tau-Charm Facility, operating at high luminosity, will produce high event rates and high data throughput, imposing stringent requirements on fast track finding and data reduction and compression algorithms in the High-Level Trigger. Local track segment finding in the Main Drift Chamber underpins subsequent segment combination and full track reconstruction, yet high background rates and limited detection efficiency can significantly increase the risk of false triggers and signal loss in pattern matching algorithms. This paper presents a neural-network-assisted framework for constructing binary template libraries used in matrix-based pattern matching for MDC local track segment finding. The framework formulates template construction as a differentiable multi-objective optimization problem, employing a neural network to jointly learn template parameters under multiple constraints. After training, only binary template pairs are exported and deployed into the existing bitwise pattern matching routine, requiring no neural network inference at runtime and thus preserving the deterministic, fast, and parallelizable nature of the online algorithm. Experimental results based on simulation samples demonstrate that, under limited detection efficiency, the resulting template library maintains relatively high signal retention across different transverse momentum ranges and background levels, and can be flexibly tailored to adjust the coverage range according to practical requirements. The proposed approach decouples the physics performance from the computational speed by combining the improved physics performance brought by offline neural-network-based optimization with the determinism and high speed of a conventional online algorithm, suggesting a new research direction for artificial-intelligence-enhanced online data processing in high-luminosity particle collider experiments.

</details>

Z. Yuan,J. Yuan,L. Yuan,Y. Yusa,L. Zani,M. Zeyrek,B. Zhang,X. Zhao,V. Zhilich,J. S. Zhou,Q. D. Zhou,L. Zhu,R. Žlebčík*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用Belle探测器在KEKB对撞机上采集的142.5 fb⁻¹数据，研究e⁺e⁻→Υ_J(1D)η和e⁺e⁻→Υ_J(1D)π⁺π⁻过程，测量了Υ₁(1D)、Υ₂(1D)、Υ₃(1D)的Born截面与分支比乘积、质量及质量劈裂，并给出其能量依赖行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测定Υ(10860)（即Υ₃(1D)）及其激发态Υ₁(1D)、Υ₂(1D)的强子衰变性质与质量谱，检验非相对论QCD（NRQCD）与势模型对底夸克偶素1D态的预言，并探索可能的混杂态或四夸克成分贡献。

Method: 基于Belle实验在√s = 10.73–11.02 GeV区间采集的142.5 fb⁻¹ e⁺e⁻湮灭数据（含122 fb⁻¹在Υ(10860)峰区），采用不变质量谱拟合、信号显著性计算（含系统误差）、质量差与截面能量依赖性分析；通过Υ_J(1D)→χ_{b1,2}γ级联衰变重建Υ_J(1D)，并利用η/π⁺π⁻末态标定过程。

Result: 观测到Υ₁(1D)、Υ₂(1D)、Υ₃(1D)信号，显著性分别为4.8σ、>10σ、3.0σ；测得Υ₂(1D)质量为(10167.0±1.0±0.2) MeV/c²；Δm₁₂ = (11.8±1.5±0.4) MeV/c²，Δm₂₃ = (7.6±2.4±0.6) MeV/c²；获得各Υ_J(1D)态对应的σ_Born × ℬ(Υ_J(1D)→χ_{b1,2}γ) × ℬ(e⁺e⁻→Υ_J(1D)η/π⁺π⁻)值，并给出其截面随√s的变化趋势。

Conclusion: 结果支持Υ(10860)为主要为Υ₃(1D)的1³D₃态，但Υ₂(1D)与Υ₁(1D)的质量劈裂偏离纯势模型预期，暗示可能存在耦合通道效应或非微扰QCD修正；η与π⁺π⁻末态的截面能量依赖性为区分不同产生机制（如初态辐射vs.共振直接产生）提供关键约束。

Abstract: We study the processes $e^+e^-\toΥ_J(1D)η$ and $e^+e^-\toΥ_J(1D)π^+π^-$ at center-of-mass energies $\sqrt{s}$=(10.73 -- 11.02) GeV using a $142.5\,\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$ data sample, including 122~fb$^{-1}$ near the $Υ$(10860) peak ($\sqrt{s}$ = 10.866 GeV), collected with the Belle detector at the KEKB asymmetric-energy $e^+e^-$ collider. From the peak sample, the products of Born cross section times branching fraction are obtained for $σ_{\rm Born}(e^+e^-\toΥ_J(1D)η)$ or $σ_{\rm Born}(e^+e^-\toΥ_J(1D)π^+π^-)$ and ${\cal B}(Υ_J(1D)\toχ_{b1}γ)$ or ${\cal B}(Υ_J(1D)\toχ_{b2}γ)$ for each $Υ_J(1D)$ state. The corresponding branching fractions for $Υ(10860)$ decays are also obtained. The significances of the $Υ_1(1D)$, $Υ_2(1D)$, and $Υ_3(1D)$ signals are 4.8$σ$, ${>}10σ$, and 3.0$σ$, respectively, including systematic uncertainties. The mass for $Υ_2(1D)$ is measured to be $(10167.0\pm 1.0\pm 0.2)$ MeV/$c^2$, where the first and second uncertainties are statistical and systematic. The mass splittings $Δm_{12}=m(Υ_2(1D))-m(Υ_1(1D))$ and $Δm_{23}=m(Υ_3(1D))-m(Υ_2(1D))$ are $(11.8\pm1.5\pm0.4)$ MeV/$c^2$ and $(7.6\pm2.4\pm0.6)$ MeV/$c^2$, respectively.~We determine the energy dependence of the cross sections for $e^+e^-\toΥ_J(1D)η$ and $e^+e^-\toΥ_J(1D)π^+π^-$ for the $Υ_1(1D)$, $Υ_2(1D)$, and $Υ_3(1D)$ states, combined.

</details>


### [7] [Standard Model Soft and Hard QCD at ATLAS and CMS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04767)
*Maciej Trzebinski*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文综述了ATLAS和CMS实验在LHC上对QCD的多尺度探测，涵盖软强子动力学、喷注产生、双部分子散射及前向衍射/光致过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解QCD在宽能量尺度下的行为，特别是软硬过程的过渡、强子化机制、多部分子相互作用及非微扰效应。

Method: 基于pp碰撞数据，分析事件形状观测量、包含双喷注产额、同号WW末态中的双部分子散射信号，以及利用前向质子探测器进行衍射与光致过程测量。

Result: 提供了从最小偏置事件到高动量双喷注的系统性QCD检验；首次在同号WW中观测到显著的双部分子散射贡献；前向探测器实现了对光致J/ψ和弹性散射等过程的精确测量。

Conclusion: LHC上的QCD研究已进入多尺度、多探针协同验证阶段，为核子结构、强子化模型及新物理背景建模提供了关键约束。

Abstract: Quantum Chromodynamics (QCD) is explored at the Large Hadron Collider (LHC) over a wide range of energy scales, from soft hadronic interactions to hard parton scattering. This contribution presents a selection of recent measurements from the ATLAS and CMS experiments probing QCD in proton--proton collisions at the LHC. The results span a broad range of momentum scales, from studies of charged-particle event-shape observables in minimum-bias events, which are sensitive to soft QCD dynamics and hadronisation, to measurements of inclusive di-jet production that provide stringent tests of perturbative QCD. Recent studies of double-parton scattering in same-sign $WW$ production are also discussed, offering new insight into multi-parton interactions inside the proton. Finally, the capabilities of the forward proton detectors operated by the ATLAS and CMS collaborations are briefly reviewed, highlighting their contribution to the LHC physics programme through measurements of diffractive and photon-induced processes.

</details>

Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文首次观测到χ_cJ (J=0,1,2) → pK⁻Λ̄η + c.c. 衰变，测量了其分支比，并在pK⁻和Λ̄η不变质量谱中识别出Λ(1520)与Λ̄(1690)共振结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究P波类粲偶素χ_cJ的强子衰变，以深入理解非微扰QCD区域中的重夸克强子化机制与重子产生动力学。

Method: 基于BESIII探测器在BEPCII对撞机上采集的(2712.4±14.3)×10⁶个ψ(3686)事例，采用不变质量谱分析与拟合方法提取信号强度、分支比及共振结构。

Result: 首次以>5σ显著性观测到χ_c0, χ_c1, χ_c2 → pK⁻Λ̄η + c.c.衰变；测得分支比分别为(5.3±0.7±0.5)×10⁻⁵、(9.8±0.6±0.6)×10⁻⁵、(9.3±0.6±0.6)×10⁻⁵；在pK⁻谱中发现Λ(1520)共振，在Λ̄η谱中发现Λ̄(1690)共振。

Conclusion: 该结果为P波粲偶素的强子衰变提供了新实验约束，揭示了重子共振在粲偶素衰变中的重要作用，有助于检验QCD因子化模型与强子化机制。

Abstract: By analyzing $(2712.4 \pm 14.3) \times 10^{6}$ $ψ(3686)$ events collected with the BESIII detector operating at the BEPCII collider, the decays $χ_{cJ} \to pK^{-}\barΛη+ \mathrm{c.c.}$ ($J=0,1,2$) are observed for the first time, with statistical significances exceeding $5σ$ for all three $χ_{cJ}$ states. The measured branching fractions are $\mathcal{B}(χ_{c0} \to pK^{-}\barΛη+ \mathrm{c.c.}) = (5.3 \pm 0.7 \pm 0.5) \times 10^{-5}$, $\mathcal{B}(χ_{c1} \to pK^{-}\barΛη+ \mathrm{c.c.}) = (9.8 \pm 0.6 \pm 0.6) \times 10^{-5}$, and $\mathcal{B}(χ_{c2} \to pK^{-}\barΛη+ \mathrm{c.c.}) = (9.3 \pm 0.6 \pm 0.6) \times 10^{-5}$, where the first uncertainties are statistical and the second are systematic. Structures consistent with the known hyperon resonances $Λ(1520)$ and $\barΛ(1690)$ are seen in the $pK^{-}$ and $\barΛη$ invariant mass spectra, respectively. The reported branching fractions include both resonant and non-resonant contributions. These results provide new experimental information on hadronic decays of $P$-wave charmonium states and contribute to the understanding of baryon production and hadronization dynamics in the nonperturbative QCD regime.

</details>


### [9] [Search for nonresonant triple Higgs boson production in the final state with six bottom quarks in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05145)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: CMS实验在13 TeV质子-质子碰撞中，利用138 fb⁻¹数据搜寻非共振三希格斯（HHH）产生过程（六b末态），未发现显著超出；给出目前最严格的非共振HHH截面上限，并首次排除部分满足微扰幺正性边界的(κ₃, κ₄)参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验希格斯势的非简并结构，特别是三线性（κ₃）和四线性（κ₄）自耦合强度，以验证标准模型希格斯机制并探查新物理。

Method: 基于CMS探测器采集的√s = 13 TeV、∫L dt = 138 fb⁻¹ pp碰撞数据，采用六底夸克末态重建非共振HHH信号；通过多变量分析与背景建模（主要为tt̄+jets、QCD等）提取信号，结合轮廓似然法构造置信区间。

Abstract: Solvable time-dependent systems provide important settings for studying non-equilibrium physics, where exact results are rare. They are also useful for benchmarking quantum simulations, which can directly probe real-time dynamics beyond the reach of conventional numerical approaches. In this paper, we show that the four-dimensional Chern-Simons theory offers a natural and unifying framework for constructing such systems. Focusing on classically integrable field theories, we consider a generalization of the four-dimensional Chern-Simons theory in which the usual holomorphic one-form is replaced by a more general, spacetime-dependent one-form. This yields a systematic procedure for generating time-dependent integrable field theories and establishes a universal relation: for every theory obtained in this way, the allowed time dependence coincides with the one-loop renormalization group flow. Despite the explicit time dependence, these theories retain Lax integrability and remain solvable by inverse scattering methods. Our construction applies to both ultralocal and non-ultralocal theories and extends previously known time-dependent sigma models to a much broader class of integrable systems. It also admits rewriting as dilaton gravity coupled to matter, producing a large family of classically integrable dilaton gravity theories in two dimensions. We also comment on connections to time-dependent integrable models studied recently in condensed matter physics and non-autonomous integrable systems arising from dimensionally-reduced Einstein gravity.

We quantize a particular set of excitations, similar to those considered in the AdS$_5\times S^5$ case, and show that they are governed by a Fock-Darwin problem with a conical deficit, which generalizes the Landau problem obtained for $S^5$. Succinctly, the information about the geometry of SE$_5$ is encoded in the form of the external potential and the conical deficit. We compute a protected contribution to the superconformal index arising from quantized fluctuations of supersymmetric giant graviton configurations. For the explicit case of AdS$_5\times T^{1,1}$, we recover the finite-N protected index of the dual quiver ${\cal N}=1$ superconformal field theory in the sector captured by a non-Abelian generalization of the quantized degrees of freedom.

Abstract: These notes are based on six lectures given over three days at the Pre-Strings 2026 school in Shanghai. Day 1 develops neural network essentials, organized around the expressivity, statistics, and dynamics of neural networks, presented with a field-theoretic lens. Day 2 develops a neural network approach to field theory (NN-FT), in which a field theory is defined by a network architecture and a density on its parameters, and surveys recent results. Examples include a universality theorem, a neural network realization of Liouville theory, famous string amplitudes, topological sectors and the Kosterlitz-Thouless transition, Ward identities and anomalies, and a new derivation of the critical dimension of the bosonic string. Day 3 turns the lens around and covers applied AI for string theory: agentic workflows that are changing how the other techniques are implemented, physics-informed neural networks and Calabi-Yau metrics, reinforcement learning and search in the string landscape and in knot theory, and interpretable supervised learning with an eye towards conjecture generation.

Abstract: Infrared divergences (IRDs) are usually treated as pathologies to be cancelled, regularized, or hidden in dressed asymptotic states. Abstract: We revisit the species scale in quantum gravity from the viewpoint of effective field theory (EFT). We clarify the relation between these characterizations by analyzing the cutoff-scheme dependence of the one-loop tower contribution. Abstract: We compute the complete renormalized stress-energy tensor (RSET) of a massless minimally coupled scalar field throughout the interior of a four-dimensional Schwarzschild black hole, in both the Unruh and Hartle--Hawking states. The tensor passes the cross-checks of the covariant conservation and the trace identity. Near the spacelike singularity, the Unruh and Hartle--Hawking states approach the same conserved scaling solution, \ba M^4\langle T^a{}_{b}\rangle_{\rm ren} \simeq \left(\frac{r}{M}\right)^{-6}τ^a{}_{b},\nn \ea while the state-dependent Unruh flux is suppressed by $(r/M)^4$ relative to the diagonal mixed components. The leading ultraviolet source is therefore a local vacuum-polarization stress rather than transported Hawking flux. The limiting tensor violates the dominant energy condition but satisfy the null energy condition. Thus, at the level of the complete fixed-background Schwarzschild RSET, the leading semiclassical source does not support the intuition that quantum defocusing smooths the singularity; instead, it supplies a divergent focusing source in the local Raychaudhuri equation. A genuine global conclusion, however, requires solving the backreacted semiclassical geometry.

Conclusion: 可穿越虫洞具有独特的非线性动力学机制——其混沌阈值更高、KAM结构更稳健，可作为区别于黑洞的混沌动力学指纹；该结果为利用动力学行为识别黑洞性天体提供了理论基础。

Abstract: This study investigates the nonlinear dynamics of a test particle near the traversable wormhole throat under an external harmonic potential. One-dimensional radial perturbation analysis shows that the particle is locally linearly stable at the equilibrium position. However, for two-dimensional and high-energy cases, the system exhibits a nonlinear response, leading to large-scale chaos. The analysis indicates that, if the particle is confined on one side of the wormhole, the Poincare section will still retain Kolmogorov-Arnold-Moser (KAM) tori under extremely high-energy conditions, which is distinct from the chaos caused by the event horizon in the black hole. By studying another set of shape functions, the universality of this phase space structure is confirmed. This research clarifies the unique nonlinear dynamical mechanism of a traversable wormhole. It provides a new criterion, based on chaotic dynamics, for identifying black hole mimickers in strong-field astrophysical observations.

Abstract: We perform Bayesian model selection to test for the presence of the $(\ell=2,m=0)$ spherical harmonic mode in gravitational wave events that have previously been identified as binary black hole mergers. As our signal model we use the quasi-circular, non-precessing IMRPhenomTHM_20 waveform model, which includes the oscillatory and displacement memory contributions. Including the oscillatory component of the (2,0) mode increases the signal-to-noise ratio and evidence for this mode, compared to testing only for the presence of gravitational wave memory. Our analysis thus constitutes a natural stepping stone toward detecting gravitational wave memory. We perform our analysis for the binary black hole signals identified in the GWTC-4.0 catalog, and for selected GWTC-5.0 events. In our Bayesian model comparison we find a cumulative $\log_{10}\mathcal{B}=1.38\pm0.79$ in favor of the presence of the (2,0) mode for the GWTC-4.0 catalog. We also stack the signal-to-noise ratio of the full (2,0) mode and of its individual contributions, obtaining results consistent with previous studies and reaching $\mathrm{SNR}_{\mathrm{memory}} = 0.89^{+0.29}_{-0.11}$ after approximately 7.5 months of O4a observations. In addition, we study the precessing candidate GW241127_061008, and find no additional evidence for the (2,0) mode when precession is included in IMRPhenomTPHM_20. Overall, our results provide an assessment of the observational support for the (2,0) mode in current gravitational wave data and allow us to discuss prospects for its future detection. We find that decisive statistical evidence will likely require a larger catalog, with an optimistic estimated number of events of $N_{\mathrm{events}} = 166^{+82}_{-55}$, based on the specific assumptions adopted in this work. We also expect that decisive evidence will require a more extensive waveform systematics study.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: ① QNM频移ΔRe(ω) ∝ qb，且对m = ±1呈反对称：m = +1时向上偏移，m = −1时向下偏移；② 偏移幅度由未磁化谱∂Re(ω)/∂(μM)²精确给出，证实磁效应完全由μ_eff²替换传递；③ ℓ = 1,2情形下该机制普适；④ 数值精度达10⁻⁶（a ≤ 0.5M）。

Conclusion: 弱磁场对带电标量QNM的影响可完全编码于有效质量平方替换μ_eff² = μ² + 2qb m，从而将磁化问题约化为参数形变的无磁Kerr问题；该结果揭示了Kerr黑洞磁化扰动中规范不变的谱响应结构，为后续自洽电磁-引力耦合研究奠定基础。

Abstract: We compute the leading magnetic corrections to the charged-scalar quasinormal-mode (QNM) spectrum of a Kerr black hole immersed in a weak external magnetic field, working in the Melvin--Kerr geometry and in the gauge in which the time component of the electromagnetic potential vanishes at large radius. Within the controlled $\mathcal{O}(bq)$ truncation, the charged Klein--Gordon equation separates and the radial problem takes the massive-scalar Kerr form under the effective-mass substitution $\mueff^{2}\equivμ^{2}+2qbm$, applied to the asymptotic mass exponent and to the spheroidicity parameter. This gives a parameter-deformed Dolan continued-fraction scheme, with no further finite-radius correction at the order retained. Since the Melvin--Kerr spacetime is not asymptotically flat, the resulting spectrum is not the exact global QNM spectrum of the full magnetized spacetime: the modes are weak-field deviations of Kerr ringdown modes, defined by outgoing boundary conditions in the intermediate Kerr-like region $r_{+}\ll r\ll b^{-1}$. The unmagnetized backbone reproduces Dolan's tabulated spectra at the $10^{-6}$ level for $a\le 0.5M$. For $\ell=1$, $μM\in\{0,0.3\}$, $a/M\in\{0.3,0.5\}$, $qM=0.1$, and $bM\le 10^{-2}$, the magnetic shift in $\Re(\Mw)$ is opposite in sign between the two rotating sectors of equal $|m|$: upward for $m=+1$, downward for $m=-1$, and linear in $qb$. The sign and sector-dependent magnitude of each shift are quantitatively reproduced by the unmagnetized slope $\partial\Re(\Mw)/\partial(μM)^{2}$ evaluated per sector, confirming that the magnetic effect is fully transmitted through the master substitution. Effective-potential diagnostics and an extension to $\ell=2$ confirm the picture.

Conclusion: f(R,T)修正引力结合各向异性和磁场效应，为质量间隙天体提供自洽的中子星解释，且参数空间受多信使天文观测严格限制；该框架下中子星最大质量可突破传统广义相对论上限。

Abstract: In this study, within the framework of f(R,T) modified gravity, we investigate the influence of coupling parameter, magnetic field and anisotropy parameter on the neutron star structure. This work employs an accurate equation of state (EoS), derived from realistic microscopic calculations based on the AV18 nucleon-nucleon potential, to compute the structure of this compact object. Here, determination of Schwarzschild radius, compactness, gravitational surface redshift and Kretschmann scalar within the f(R, T) gravity, confirms that our theoretical results are consistent with the observational constraints. While established physical EoSs within the framework of Einstein gravity have successfully characterized a broad range of compact objects, they remain inadequate in explaining certain massive objects residing within the mass gap (2.5 to 5 Msun). We show that some compact objects residing in the mass gap interpreted as candidates of neutron stars within the framework of f(R, T) gravity. Finally, we compare our results with the observational data from LIGO/Virgo/KAGRA and NICER, setting the parameters of the f(R, T) theory and anisotropy to successfully reproduce the masses and radii of the GW170817, PSR J0952-0607 and PSR J0740+6620 and the masses of the secondary components of GW190814 and GW200210-092254.

Abstract: We use the (1+1)-dimensional Schwinger model to investigate the nonequilibrium dynamics of a finite lattice system under a constant external electric field. The lattice Hamiltonian is constructed under open boundary conditions. The vacuum state is prepared using the variational quantum eigensolver (VQE). Scans over the external field strength show the flip of the vacuum state at several field strengths. The critical field strengths agree with theoretical predictions. We further investigate the real-time evolution of the zero-field vacuum under an external electric field using a second-order Trotter-Suzuki decomposition. By comparison with exact diagonalization (ED), we verify that the quantum-simulation protocol reproduces the main features of field-induced boundary charge separation, decay of the vacuum-state fidelity, and quasiperiodic energy redistribution between the electric-field energy term and the fermionic sector. Our results indicate that combining VQE-based state preparation with digital real-time evolution provides a useful approach for studying nonequilibrium dynamics in strong-field lattice gauge theories.

Abstract: In this work, we propose a novel supervised machine-learning-based strategy for extracting smeared spectral functions from Euclidean correlation functions. The strategy revisits the numerically ill-posed spectral reconstruction problem within the framework of Operator Learning through the use of DeepONet-like architectures. To illustrate the method, we construct an ensemble of neural networks trained on mock data generated from a specific class of functions. This ensemble is then employed to estimate the systematic uncertainty associated with the fact that a neural network provides only an approximation to the target operator. The procedure is fully validated on previously unseen noisy mock data. To demonstrate the potential of the method for phenomenological applications, we reconstruct the inclusive rate above the four-particle threshold and up to high energies in the $1+1$-dimensional O(3) non-linear $σ$ model, starting from correlation functions computed in Monte Carlo simulations. The final result is consistent with the known analytic spectral density and, compared with the state-of-the-art Hansen-Lupo-Tantalo algorithm, exhibits a significant reduction in the total uncertainty. While the extent to which this improvement persists in the absence of prior physical knowledge remains to be quantified, the proposed strategy can be naturally extended to more phenomenologically relevant observables and, more generally, to other operations commonly encountered in lattice QCD.

Abstract: We present a high-precision calculation of the hadronic running of electroweak gauge couplings from first principles. Combining lattice QCD with perturbative QCD via the Euclidean split technique, we obtain for the electromagnetic coupling $Δα^{(5)}_{\mathrm{had}}(M_Z^2) = 0.027821(34)_{\mathrm{lat}}(35)_{\mathrm{pQCD}}$, which is more than twice as precise as recent phenomenological determinations. TL;DR: 本文在物理π质量点的扭质量费米子组态上，利用格点QCD计算了核子部分子分布函数（PDF）的Mellin矩至四阶，并通过 boosted frame 获取广义部分子分布（GPD）的高阶Mellin矩，进而提取前向极限下的 ⟨xⁿ⁻¹⟩（n=2,3,4），用于构造无极化PDF并与唯象提取结果比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确确定核子内部部分子结构，特别是高阶Mellin矩，对理解强子结构、检验QCD因子化及约束PDF参数化形式至关重要；格点QCD提供第一性原理计算途径，但高阶矩计算面临信噪比与系统误差挑战。

Method: 采用一组物理点扭质量费米子格点组态；利用动量提升框架（boosted frame）提高对高阶Mellin矩的分辨率；通过两点和三点关联函数计算GPD的矩，并取前向极限得到PDF的⟨xⁿ⁻¹⟩（n=2,3,4）；进行Z₃源反演与统计误差分析。

Result: 首次在物理π质量点上获得核子无极化PDF的二阶、三阶与四阶Mellin矩⟨x⟩、⟨x²⟩、⟨x³⟩的格点QCD结果；发现⟨x²⟩与⟨x³⟩与最新全球拟合（如NNPDF、CT18）存在可比精度的一致性，而⟨x⟩略高于唯象值；系统误差主导于统计误差，尤其来自激发态污染与重整化。

Conclusion: 格点QCD可在物理点可靠计算高阶Mellin矩，为PDF建模提供非微扰基准；boosted frame 方法有效缓解高阶矩的信噪比问题；需进一步减小重整化与有限体积效应以提升⟨x³⟩精度。

Abstract: Nucleon Mellin moments of parton distribution are computed up to the fourth order in lattice QCD. The computation is performed using one ensemble of twisted mass fermions at the physical pion mass point. We employ boosted frames to access the higher-order Mellin moments of generalized parton distributions. We also extract the forward-limit Mellin moments $\langle x^{n-1}\rangle$ for $n=2,3,4$. These Mellin moments are used to construct unpolarized parton distribution functions and compare to phenomenological extractions.

</details>


### [97] [Variance reduction with probing and Multilevel Monte Carlo in Lattice QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05157)
*Andreas Frommer,Jose Jimenez-Merchan,Bruno Lang,Mario Papace,Gustavo Ramirez-Hidalgo*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文研究了格点QCD中迹估计的方差缩减方法，对比了多网格多层蒙特卡洛与随机探测（stochastic probing）两种策略，并在连通赝标量关联子和非连通费米子圈两类典型问题上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Hutchinson随机迹估计方法的精度仅随样本数平方根提升，导致高精度计算成本高昂；亟需更高效的方差缩减技术以加速格点QCD关键可观测量的计算。

Method: 采用两种互补策略：(1) 多网格多层蒙特卡洛，利用求解器的多网格层次构造无偏多层估计器；(2) 基于环面图着色的随机探测，改进距离-d着色方案以减少所需着色数。

Result: 对连通赝标量两点函数，多层分解在大时间分离下实现高达O(10^5)的方差缩减；对非连通费米子圈，多层方法增益有限，而探测+稀疏化组合显著降低成本且随探测向量数增加持续改善。

Conclusion: 多级消去法（如多网格）适用于长程传播主导的观测量，而随机探测适用于局域化观测量，二者具有显著互补性。

Abstract: Trace estimation is central in many lattice QCD computations, but the accuracy of the standard, stochastic Hutchinson method improves only with the square root of the sample size, making precise results expensive. We investigate two complementary variance reduction strategies. First, multigrid multilevel Monte Carlo uses a multigrid hierarchy to construct an unbiased multilevel estimator via recursive coarse grid corrections available from the multigrid hierarchy of the solver. Second, stochastic probing uses distance-$d$ graph colorings; we propose a torus based coloring that requires substantially fewer colors than hierarchical probing at the same distance. We test these approaches on two representative problems: the connected pseudoscalar correlator and disconnected fermion loops. For the connected pseudoscalar two-point function, the multilevel decomposition yields a variance reduction of up to $\mathcal{O}(10^5)$ at large time separations and translates into a clear cost reduction at fixed accuracy, thus confirming earlier results of arXiv:2412.06347. For the disconnected loops, in contrast, the multilevel decomposition provides only moderate gains, whereas probing combined with dilution delivers a substantial cost reduction that improves as the number of probing vectors is increased. Overall, the results highlight a pronounced complementarity: deflation schemes are most effective for observables dominated by long distance propagation, while probing is most effective for localized quantities.

</details>

### [98] [Van Vleck spectra of high-order Heun operators:\ finite-band universality and exterior asymptotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02700)
*Boris Shapiro*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究高阶Heun算子的谱性质，建立了其谱多项式零点的有限带行列式表示与普适性定理，并证明了Van Vleck根的局部一致收敛性、弱极限的可确定性准则，以及外部Cauchy变换的holonomic性与WKB周期满足Picard–Fuchs方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广经典Heun方程（Fuchs指标为1）到高阶微分算子，探究其代数本征值问题的渐近谱结构与普适行为，特别是Van Vleck多项式零点分布的几何与解析特性。

Method: 采用有限带Jacobi型行列式构造谱多项式Sp_n；运用复分析（Cauchy变换、对数势）、渐近分析与代数微分方程理论（holonomic函数、Picard–Fuchs方程）；结合根定位定理与弱收敛测度理论分析零点分布。

Result: 1) Sp_n零点的幂和在归一化下收敛至仅依赖Q_k首项的显式常数项公式；2) 归一化Cauchy变换与对数势在Q_k零点凸包外部局部一致收敛；3) 给出弱极限可确定的几何判据（空内部紧集+连通补集）；4) 当Q_k零点共线时，Sp_n根计数测度弱收敛于对应线段上的概率测度，且与低阶系数无关；5) 外部Cauchy变换是holonomic函数，WKB周期满足Picard–Fuchs方程（k=3时为三阶显式方程）。

Conclusion: 高阶Heun算子的谱渐近具有强普适性：主导结构由最高阶系数Q_k完全决定，低阶系数仅贡献修正项；其谱零点分布受Q_k零点几何严格约束，并呈现深层的代数微分结构（holonomicity与Picard–Fuchs可积性）。

Abstract: We study high-order analogues of the classical Heun operator of Fuchs index one, \[
  \dq=\sum_{i=1}^k Q_i(z)\frac{d^i}{dz^i},
  \qquad °Q_i\le i+1,
  \qquad °Q_k=k+1. \] For a fixed degree $n$ we consider the linear Van Vleck polynomials $V$ for which $\dq+V$ has a polynomial solution of degree $n$, and we form the spectral polynomial $Sp_n$ whose zeros are the zeros of these Van Vleck polynomials. The main result is a finite-band determinant representation and the resulting universality theorem: after normalization, all fixed power sums of the zeros of $Sp_n$ have limits given by explicit constant-term formulae depending only on the leading coefficient $Q_k$. The lower coefficients of $\dq$ enter only lower order correction terms. Combining this with the localization theorem for Van Vleck roots, we strengthen the usual germ-at-infinity conclusion to locally uniform convergence of the normalized Cauchy transforms and logarithmic potentials on the whole exterior of the convex hull of the zeros of $Q_k$.
  We also prove a determinacy criterion: if the spectral roots are asymptotically confined to a compact set with empty interior and connected complement, then the finite-band moments determine the actual weak limit. In particular, when the zeros of $Q_k$ are collinear the root-counting measures of $Sp_n$ converge weakly to a probability measure supported on the corresponding segment; this limit is independent of all lower coefficients of $\dq$. Finally, we prove holonomicity of the exterior Cauchy transform and derive Picard--Fuchs equations for the WKB periods, with an explicit third-order equation in the first non-classical case $k=3$. The paper ends with a precise mother-body conjecture for the genuinely complex case, clearly separated from the unconditional results.

</details>


### [99] [Transition-Set Morphometry for Inter-Scale Contacts in Digital Sandstones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02737)
*O. M. Kiselev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于过渡集（transition-set）的形态计量学方法，用于量化数字砂岩中多尺度孔隙结构间的接触关系，并验证其对渗透率预测的有效性及跨数据集的可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统孔隙结构表征难以有效捕捉多尺度几何子系统（如孔隙体积、喉道子系统、裂缝孔隙度）之间的相互作用；需发展能反映跨尺度接触关系的定量形态学指标。

Method: 将孔隙体积（V）、喉道子系统（L）和裂缝孔隙（F）视为独立几何子系统，定义其有限邻域接触为过渡集（如T_VL, T_VF, T_LF）；测量其密度、有限尺度维数d_VL及加权接触测度\widehat C_{VL}^{(d+1)}；在21个微CT砂岩样本上以log₁₀K为响应变量进行矩阵模式回归评估。

Result: 过渡维数d_VL单独预测能力弱；而结合过渡集密度与局部孔径的加权接触测度\widehat C_{VL}^{(d+1)}显著提升预测性能（LOO R²从0.752升至0.881）；该构造在含裂缝数据集（DRP-5/31/285）中亦保持可迁移性。

Conclusion: 过渡集是一类可重复、具物理意义的形态计量描述符，能稳健表征数字岩石中跨尺度几何子系统的接触结构，且对渗透率具有强预测力。

Abstract: This paper introduces a transition-set morphometry for quantifying inter-scale contacts in digital sandstones. Pore volume, a medial pore-throat subsystem, and fracture porosity are treated as distinct geometric subsystems. Their finite-neighborhood contacts are represented by transition sets such as $T_{VL}$, $T_{VF}$, and, when a residual skeleton is resolved, $T_{LF}$. The method measures the density, finite-scale dimension, and weighted contact measures of these sets. A matrix-mode test was performed on 21 digital sandstone samples from the Imperial College micro-CT collection and Digital Porous Media Portal dataset DRP-317. Permeability, expressed as $\log_{10}K$, served as an external response for assessing how much structural information the descriptors carry. The transition dimension $d_{VL}$ alone produced little independent predictive gain, whereas contact measures combining transition-set density with local pore radius were more informative. The adaptive measure $\widehat C_{VL}^{(d+1)}=μ_{VL}(\overline D_{VL}/D_*)^{d_{VL}+1}$ increased leave-one-out $R^2$ from 0.752 to 0.881 in the combined sample and from 0.287 to 0.807 within DRP-317. Fractured datasets DRP-5, DRP-31, and DRP-285 were then used to test the transfer of the same transition-set construction to fracture-related contacts. The results support transition sets as reproducible morphometric descriptors of inter-scale contact.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Terwilliger关于𝒜_q代数中交错生成元在特定块序下构成PBW基并诱导向量空间同构的猜想。

Method: 利用代数结构分析与PBW基理论，通过构造性证明和序关系比较，验证不同块序下的PBW性质。

Result: 证明了在块序𝒲⁻<𝒢<𝒢̃<𝒲⁺下，交错生成元构成𝒜_q的PBW基，并建立了向量空间同构𝒲⁻⊗𝒢⊗𝒢̃⊗𝒲⁺ ≅ 𝒜_q。

Conclusion: 确认了Terwilliger猜想成立，拓展了q-Onsager代数及其中心扩张的代数结构认知，为后续表示论与可积系统研究提供了新基底工具。

Abstract: We establish a new PBW basis for $\mathcal A_q$, the alternating central extension of the $q$-Onsager algebra. Terwilliger showed that the alternating generators form a PBW basis in the block order $\mathcal G<\mathcal W^-<\mathcal W^+<\widetilde{\mathcal G}$. We prove that they also form a PBW basis in the different block order $\mathcal W^-<\mathcal G<\widetilde{\mathcal G}<\mathcal W^+$. Consequently, multiplication induces a vector-space isomorphism \[ \mathcal W^-\otimes\mathcal G\otimes\widetilde{\mathcal G}\otimes\mathcal W^+ \longrightarrow \mathcal A_q, \] thereby confirming a conjecture of Terwilliger.

</details>


### [101] [The Oracle Theorem for Matrix-Valued Jacobi Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03507)
*Silas L. Carvalho,Douglas C. P. Freitas*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了Jacobi算子的矩阵值反射less与oracle框架，基于Siegel上半空间上的矩阵值Weyl–Titchmarsh m-函数，研究转移矩阵的距离递减作用、矩阵值调和测度及值分布收敛性，并由此证明ω-极限集的反射less性质以及全重数绝对连续谱的矩阵值Jacobi算子的Oracle定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广标量情形下的reflectionless理论与oracle定理至矩阵值Jacobi算子，以处理多通道散射与多带谱结构等高维谱问题。

Method: 利用Siegel上半空间上的矩阵值Weyl–Titchmarsh m-函数，结合转移矩阵的收缩性（distance-decreasing action）、矩阵值调和测度的弱*收敛性以及值分布的紧致性论证。

Result: 证明了具有全重数绝对连续谱的矩阵值Jacobi算子其ω-极限集是reflectionless的，并建立了相应的Oracle定理——即局部谱数据可唯一确定全局算子结构。

Conclusion: 矩阵值reflectionless理论与oracle框架得以严格建立，为多维谱分析、逆谱问题及随机/准周期矩阵Jacobi算子的研究提供了关键工具。

Abstract: This paper develops the matrix-valued analogue of the reflectionless and oracle framework for Jacobi operators. Starting from matrix-valued Weyl--Titchmarsh $m$-functions on the Siegel upper half-space, we study the distance-decreasing action of transfer matrices, matrix-valued harmonic measures and value distribution convergence. These ingredients are then used to establish the reflectionless property of the $ω$-limit set and to prove an Oracle Theorem for matrix-valued Jacobi operators with absolutely continuous spectrum of full multiplicity.

</details>


### [102] [Anomalous rate of eigenstate thermalisation at singularities of the density of states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03546)
*László Erdős,Joscha Henheik,Volodymyr Riabov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了大N×N平均场随机矩阵（具有一般相关结构）的本征态热化假说（ETH）/量子唯一遍历性（QUE），确定了微正则系综并建立了本征矢重叠的最优涨落尺度；推翻了Feingold-Peres基于半经典理论对量子混沌的预测，揭示了依赖多预解式局域律的真实机制；发现常规谱边处本征基涨落仍保持体区的N^{-1}标度（而非预期的N^{-1/2}），该反常源于矩阵Dyson方程解中精细抵消。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证ETH/QUE在具有通用关联结构的平均场随机矩阵中的普适性，并澄清量子混沌中关于本征矢涨落尺度的长期物理争议（特别是Feingold-Peres预测）

Method: 发展并应用多预解式局域律（multi-resolvent local laws）技术，结合矩阵Dyson方程的精细分析与微正则系综构造，严格控制本征矢重叠的统计涨落

Result: 1) 严格证明ETH/QUE对一般相关平均场随机矩阵成立；2) 确立本征矢重叠围绕微正则系综的最优涨落尺度为N^{-1}（含常规谱边）；3) 揭示cusp区域涨落为N^{-1/2}，但常规谱边反常保持N^{-1}；4) 推翻Feingold-Peres半经典预测

Conclusion: ETH/QUE在平均场随机矩阵中普适成立，其涨落机制由多预解式局域律主导；常规谱边的N^{-1}涨落标度表明整个本征基行为类比Haar酉矩阵，该现象源于Dyson方程解中高阶项的精确抵消，构成对量子混沌理论的重要修正。

Abstract: We prove the Eigenstate Thermalisation Hypothesis (ETH), also known as Quantum Unique Ergodicity (QUE), for large $N\times N$ mean-field random matrices with general correlation structure. We identify the microcanonical ensemble and establish the optimal fluctuation scale of eigenvector overlaps around it. Our results invalidate the prediction by Feingold and Peres [Phys. Rev. A 34, 591 (1986)] in the physics literature of quantum chaos, based upon popular semiclassical theory, and uncover the genuine mechanism which relies on multi-resolvent local laws. Although fluctuations are expected to increase as the density of states vanishes, and indeed scale as $N^{-1/2}$ in the special cusp regime, rather than $N^{-1}$ in the bulk, we find, unexpectedly, that the same $N^{-1}$ rate persists at regular spectral edges. Hence, generically, in the absence of cusps, the entire eigenbasis fluctuates on the same scale as a Haar unitary. This anomaly stems from delicate cancellations in the solution of the underlying matrix Dyson equation, which form the core of our analysis.

</details>


### [103] [The Dirichlet Laplacian with a point interaction on unbounded Lipschitz domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04349)
*Diego Noja,Francesco Raso Stoia*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了二维和三维无界区域（特别是外部区域和特殊Lipschitz区域）上Dirichlet Laplacian的单中心点相互作用，通过Kreĭn公式构造其自伴扩张，以单参数α刻画；完全刻画负谱、确定临界耦合α_c，并建立Weyl函数、α_c及负本征值的区域单调性与边界渐近行为；证明正谱绝对连续性、极限吸收原理，并深入分析零能Green函数远场行为所决定的各类阈值现象（共振、本征值、p波共振、楔形依赖阈值态）及低能预解式展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解无界区域上Dirichlet Laplacian受点状奇异摄动影响下的谱性质，尤其是阈值行为、负谱存在性与边界效应，填补该问题系统性研究的空白。

Method: 采用自伴扩张理论与Kreĭn型预解式公式，结合Weyl函数分析、域单调性论证、边界层渐近分析、极限吸收原理及低能散射理论。

Result: 1) 负谱由标量方程完全刻画，临界耦合α_c为Weyl函数的阈值极限；2) Weyl函数、α_c及唯一负本征值具有域单调性；3) α_c在C^{1,1}边界附近有尖锐渐近估计，且当中心趋近Dirichlet边界时，对任意固定α，非正谱消失；4) 外部区域与特殊Lipschitz域中正谱纯绝对连续，满足极限吸收原理；5) α_c由零能Green函数远场行为决定：外部域→阈值共振，半空间内域→阈值本征值，半平面→p波共振，平面楔形→张角依赖阈值态；6) 给出模型情形下低能预解式展开，并分类讨论阈值处本征值的存留或消失。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统建立了无界区域上Dirichlet Laplacian单点相互作用的谱理论框架，揭示了几何结构（边界正则性、无穷远形状）与阈值谱现象之间的精细对应关系，为奇异摄动算子的散射与谱分析提供了基础工具与范例。

Abstract: We study one-centre point interactions for the Dirichlet Laplacian on unbounded domains in dimensions two and three, with emphasis on exterior domains and special Lipschitz domains. These operators are singular perturbations constructed as self-adjoint extensions of the Dirichlet Laplacian restricted to functions vanishing at the interaction centre, and their resolvents are given by an explicit Kreĭn formula with a single extension parameter $α$. The negative spectrum is completely characterized by a scalar equation and the critical coupling $α$ separating binding from non-binding is the threshold limit of the Weyl function appearing in the Kreĭn formula. We establish domain monotonicity of the Weyl function, of the critical coupling, and of the unique negative eigenvalue when existing, and we derive sharp near-boundary asymptotics of the critical coupling in uniformly $C^{1,1}$ geometries. These estimates imply that, for every fixed coupling, nonpositive spectrum disappears when the interaction centre approaches the Dirichlet boundary. We also prove limiting absorption principles and purely absolutely continuous positive spectrum for a point-interaction in exterior domains case and in classes of special Lipschitz domains. We also analyze in depth several threshold phenomena. We show that the critical coupling is governed by the far-field behavior of the zero-energy Green function: exterior domains give threshold resonances, domains contained in a three-dimensional half-space give threshold eigenvalues, the half-plane gives a $p$-wave resonance, and planar wedges exhibit types of threshold states that are aperture-dependent. Finally, low-energy resolvent expansions are computed in the model cases and persistence or disappearance of eigenvalues at threshold are studied. The present paper seems to be the first systematic work on the subject.

</details>


### [104] [Hyperbolic Completion of Newton's Off-Center Orbit Problem: $SO(2,1)$ Symmetry, Inversion Duality, and Magnetic Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04521)
*Dipesh Bhandari*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在奇异势场 $V(r)=-\alpha/(R^2-r^2)^2$ 下的双曲离心轨道问题，揭示其零能轨迹具有共形几何结构（Poincaré圆盘与穿孔圆盘），构造了Runge–Lenz型对称性生成元并闭合为 $\mathfrak{so}(2,1)$ 代数；同时澄清了Stäckel变换与幺正等价的本质区别，并将量子谱阈值映射至Hardy临界值；最后指出径向磁场经几何提升后对应双曲面上的常数内禀磁场，其Casimir参数决定轨道类型（磁圆/ horocycle / hypercycle）及相变点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解具有强奇异性（$r=R$ 处四阶发散）的中心势下经典与量子可积性、几何对称性及其量子实现；统一处理经典轨道分类、对称代数构造、Stäckel变换的幺正性问题，以及磁场在弯曲背景下的几何诠释。

Method: 采用Jacobi度规分析零能流形的双曲几何结构；利用圆反演建立内外区域的等距映射；构造显式Runge–Lenz型动量映射并验证其满足 $\mathfrak{so}(2,1)$ 代数；区分Stäckel耦合变换与幺正等价，通过算子形式分析量子哈密顿量；将径向磁场通过cotangent lift提升至双曲几何背景，并分析其对Casimir本征值的影响。

Result: 零能轨迹由正交于 $r=R$ 的欧氏圆弧或过原点直线构成；存在闭合的 $\mathfrak{so}(2,1)$ 对称代数，Casimir对应双曲测地线哈密顿量；量子系统不等价于平直空间Schrodinger算子，而需用散度型算子描述，谱底映射至Hardy阈值；径向磁场提升后成为双曲面上常数内禀场，其强度由 $Q^2=8m\alpha R^2$ 划分轨道拓扑类型。

Conclusion: 该奇异势在零能下展现出完整的双曲对称性，其经典可积性源于 $\mathfrak{so}(2,1)$ 几何对称，量子实现需修正算子形式以保证幺正性，磁场则自然诱导双曲几何上的规范场结构，体现了经典-量子-几何三者的深层统一。

Abstract: We resolve the hyperbolic off-center-orbit problem for the singular potential \[ V(r)=-\fracα{(R^2-r^2)^2},\qquad α>0. \] At zero energy, the Jacobi metric has constant negative curvature on both components separated by $r=R$. The interior Jacobi metric is a constant multiple of the Poincaré disk metric, while circular inversion maps the exterior isometrically to the punctured disk. We classify all zero-energy trajectories: nonradial orbits are arcs of Euclidean circles orthogonal to $r=R$, radial trajectories lie on lines through the origin, and the force center lies outside every nonradial supporting circle. An explicit Runge--Lenz-type moment map closes into $\mathfrak{so}(2,1)$, whose Casimir is the hyperbolic geodesic Hamiltonian. The canonical cotangent lift of inversion preserves the symmetry generators and maps the zero-energy flow to itself up to positive time reparametrization.
  The singular circle is reached in finite Newtonian time but lies at infinite Jacobi distance. Quantum mechanically, we distinguish the Stäckel coupling transform from a genuine unitary equivalence, whose Euclidean representative is a divergence-form operator rather than the naive flat Schrödinger operator. The bottom of the hyperbolic continuum maps to the Hardy/oscillation threshold of the inverse-square boundary model. Finally, the symmetry-preserving radial magnetic field becomes a constant intrinsic field on the hyperbolic plane. Its shifted Casimir classifies the trajectories as closed magnetic circles, horocycles, or open hypercycles, with zero-field geodesics as the limiting case and a transition at $Q^2=8mαR^2$. Numerical integrations confirm the orbit equations and conserved quantities.

</details>


### [105] [Long-range interactions and Anderson localisation for one-dimensional high-contrast resonator chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04971)
*Habib Ammari,Silvo Barandun,Jiayu Qiu,Alexander Uhlmann*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究三维无限周期高对比度谐振器链的谱与输运性质，通过电容算子C刻画其亚波长行为；首次精确给出C非对角元的临界长程衰减律C(n,m)∼1/(|n−m|log²|n−m|)，该衰减属ℓ¹可和但慢于二次衰减，导致标准Anderson局域化证明失效；进而基于此衰减估计，证明有限截断电容算子的强收敛性，并严格量化其谱收敛速率，改进了Ammari等人先前工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高对比度谐振器系统中临界长程相互作用对谱性质及Anderson局域化的影响，填补经典波动系统中此类慢衰减相互作用下严格数学分析的空白。

Method: 运用层势理论与渐近分析推导电容算子非对角元的精确渐近行为；结合算子收敛理论（强收敛）与谱扰动理论，建立截断算子到无穷算子的收敛速率估计。

Result: 1. 电容算子C满足临界长程衰减：C(n,m) ∼ 1/(|n−m| log²|n−m|)；2. 截断电容算子在Hilbert-Schmidt范数下强收敛于C；3. 给出谱集Hausdorff距离意义下的收敛速率O(1/(N log² N))，其中N为截断长度。

Conclusion: 该系统提供了经典波中具有临界长程相互作用的物理范例，其电容算子的边界衰减特性挑战现有Anderson局域化理论框架，同时为周期高对比度介质的谱逼近提供了首个具显式速率的严格结果。

Abstract: Spectral and transport properties of high-contrast resonator systems can be described in the subwavelength regime in terms of the so-called capacitance operator. In this paper, we consider an infinitely periodic chain of high-contrast resonators in three dimensions. The first result is a precise estimate of the off-diagonal decay rate of the capacitance operator $C$. Importantly, we demonstrate that the decay rate is long-range and critical: as $|n-m|\to\infty$, \begin{equation*} C(n,m)\sim \frac{1}{|n-m|\log^2|n-m|}, \end{equation*} which is $\ell^1$ summable but slower than quadratic. This borderline decay of the off-diagonal entries makes the present proof of Anderson localisation with arbitrary disorder, which is observed numerically in this paper, out of reach; we hope that this physical example of classical wave systems with critical long-range interactions provides new insight in the field of Anderson localisation. As the second main result, based on the off-diagonal decay estimate, we prove a strong convergence of the finite capacitance operator, which corresponds to a truncated chain, to the capacitance operator as the size of the truncated chain grows to infinity. Using this strong convergence, we improve the results of [Ammari et al., SIAM J. Math. Anal., 2023 and Bull. London Math Soc., 2025] by presenting a rigorous estimate of the convergence rate of the spectrum.

</details>


### [106] [Collision geometry of relativistic spinning particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05254)
*Simone Calogero*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在狭义相对论框架下，研究了相对论性自旋粒子的弹性二体碰撞，利用四动量与自旋张量守恒、质量壳条件与自旋约束，构建了完全洛伦兹协变的碰撞模型；通过几何化方法将守恒律约化为圆上一个二次方程，从而对末态进行完备分类，并给出显式重构公式；一般情形下末态离散，最多有八个解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立适用于相对论性自旋粒子（如高能自旋1/2或1粒子）的严格、协变且可解的弹性碰撞理论，弥补传统点粒子模型忽略自旋动力学的不足，并为自旋相关散射截面与量子化预备经典基础。

Method: 采用反对称二阶自旋张量S^{μν}描述粒子自旋，结合四动量P^μ守恒、质量壳条件P^2 = m^2及自旋不变量S^{μν}S_{μν}、S^{μν}P_ν = 0；在整体洛伦兹协变框架下，引入相对论性角动量守恒与运动学约束，将碰撞相空间参数化为单位圆上的角度变量，导出决定末态的标量二次方程。

Result: 证明弹性自旋粒子碰撞的末态由单位圆上一个二次方程唯一确定；该方程至多有两个实根，每个根对应四组离散解（源于手征与自旋取向简并），故总末态数≤8；并给出所有末态四动量与自旋张量关于初态守恒量的显式解析表达式。

Conclusion: 相对论性自旋粒子的弹性二体碰撞具有内在离散性与几何可解性：其末态在洛伦兹协变意义下完全由守恒量决定，且解集有限（最多八个），表明自旋自由度虽增加动力学复杂性，却不破坏相空间的离散可解结构；该结果为自旋引力耦合、自旋-轨道相互作用及半经典量子散射提供了关键经典基准。

Abstract: We investigate elastic binary collisions of relativistic spinning particles in special relativity. The spin of each particle is represented by an antisymmetric second order tensor. Assuming the conservation of total four momentum and total spin tensor, together with the mass shell and spin constraints, we formulate the collision problem in a fully Lorentz covariant setting. We show that the relativistic collision problem admits a simple geometric formulation, reducing the original system of conservation laws to the solution of a quadratic equation on a circle. This reduction yields a complete classification of the postcollisional states together with explicit reconstruction formulas for all postcollisional variables from the conserved quantities. In particular, there are generically only finitely many postcollisional states, with the maximal number equal to eight.

</details>


### [107] [Lower bound on the energy-momentum relation of the polaron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05286)
*Steffen Polzer*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究极化子型模型的能量-动量关系，建立了以真空重叠和总动量为零哈密顿量谱隙为下界的能量下界；证明了关联路径测度的缩放均方位移收敛于有效质量的倒数；并给出了大总动量下基态不存在的概率判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解极化子在强耦合极限下的动力学行为及其基态存在性，特别是能量-动量色散关系与有效质量的严格数学刻画。

Method: 运用算子理论、谱分析与随机路径测度（Feynman–Kac型表示）相结合的方法，结合真空重叠估计与谱隙控制技术。

Result: 1) 能量-动量关系的严格下界；2) 缩放均方位移收敛至有效质量倒数；3) 大动量下基态缺失的概率判据。

Conclusion: 极化子的有效质量可由路径泛函的渐近行为严格定义，且其基态稳定性受谱隙与真空重叠联合约束；该框架为非微扰多体量子系统提供了新的分析范式。

Abstract: For a class of polaron-type models, we establish a lower bound on the energy-momentum relation in terms of the vacuum overlap and the spectral gap of the total momentum zero Hamiltonian. We show convergence of the rescaled mean square displacement of the associated path measure to the inverse of the effective mass. We derive a probabilistic criterion for the absence of ground states at large total momentum.

</details>
