<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决标准DFSZ模型中PQ对称性破缺方向依赖于tanβ = v_u/v_d的问题，消除对电弱真空比值的敏感性；同时统一轴子暗物质、轻中微子质量生成与暴胀机制，并确保轴子与Z玻色子Goldstone无混合以满足实验约束。

Method: 引入右手中微子N_R以实现跷跷板机制并赋予PQ对称性非平凡荷分配；构建含两个Higgs二重态与一个标量单态φ的扩展标量扇区；通过幺正基变换对角化标量质量矩阵，分离物理态（h, H, A, H±, Φ）并显式消除a–G_Z混合；计算轴子有效拉氏量，区分Yukawa型（∝ m_f ∂_μ a / f_a）与反常导数型（∝ (α/4π) a FẼμν F^μν / f_a）耦合；数值扫描参数空间，约束κ_λ = λ_{hhh}^{SM+NP}/λ_{hhh}^{SM}。

Result: PQ变换简化为仅由PQ_φ决定；轴子-光子耦合确定为DFSZ-I类（E/N = 8/3）；新标量质量谱可调至EW尺度（H±, A ∼ 150 GeV），而Φ超重（∼ inflation scale）；轴子无Z-Goldstone污染；κ_λ在所选参数下满足|κ_λ − 1| < 0.3（LHC Run 2约束）。

Conclusion: 该模型实现了PQ对称性与电弱对称性的解耦，提升了DFSZ框架的自然性与可检验性；轴子性质由单态荷唯一确定，新标量可被LHC直接探测，三线性Higgs耦合成为关键新物理探针；为轴子、中微子质量与暴胀提供统一框架。

Abstract: We show that with the introduction of new right-handed neutrinos $N_R$, the Peccei--Quinn ($PQ$) transformation in the DFSZ (Dine--Fischler--Srednicki--Zhitnitsky) model depends only on the $PQ$ charge of the scalar singlet $φ$, namely $PQ_φ$, and is independent of the sine and cosine of $\arctan(v_u/v_d)$. The model consists of four new scalars: a charged Higgs boson $H^\pm$, a CP-odd scalar $A$, and two CP-even scalars with masses at the EW scale (of a few hundred GeV), and a superheavy inflaton $Φ$. The scalar fields have been presented in the form of unitary matrices, in which there is no mixing between the axion and the Goldstone boson $G_Z$. As a result, there are two kinds of couplings between the axion and fermions, namely Yukawa-like interactions and anomaly-induced ones associated with derivative axion couplings. {The model belongs to the DFSZ I kind since the axion - photon coupling with $\fr E N = \fr 8 3$.} The trilinear Higgs self-coupling is investigated. We have shown that, in the parameter region where new scalar fields such as the singly charged Higgs boson $H^\pm$ and the CP-odd scalar $A$ have masses around 150 GeV and the vacuum expectation value of one Higgs doublet ($v_u$) is of the order of a few GeV, the coupling modifier $κ_λ$-a probe of new physics-is consistent with the current experimental constraints.

</details>


### [137] [Quantum interference effects enhanced in $π^+p$ femtoscopic correlation functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04351)
*Zhi-Wei Liu,Jun-Xu Lu,Duo-Lun Ge,Li-Sheng Geng*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过Koonin-Pratt公式结合高斯源与数据驱动的πN分波振幅，成功拟合ALICE在13 TeV pp碰撞中测得的π⁺p动量关联函数；揭示140 MeV/c峰源于入射波与散射波的量子干涉，而非传统强相互作用效应，从而解决长期存在的峰位偏移之谜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释ALICE实验中π⁺p femtoscopic关联函数在相对动量k≈140 MeV/c处异常峰位的物理起源，特别是其随横质量m_T变化的行为，即所谓的'peak-shift puzzle'。

Method: 采用Koonin-Pratt反演公式，构建包含高斯空间源分布的模型，并耦合数据驱动的πN弹性散射部分波振幅（含S₁₁、P₃₃等通道），同时显式计入Δ(1232)⁺⁺→π⁺p衰变的非共振贡献；通过加权叠加散射与衰变两机制的关联函数，拟合不同m_T区间的实验数据。

Result: 成功复现实验观测的π⁺p关联函数及其m_T依赖性；识别出k≈140 MeV/c峰源于入射波与π⁺p散射波之间的量子干涉（即Koonin-Pratt公式中的相干项），而k≈220 MeV/c峰对应Δ(1232)⁺⁺衰变动力学；两贡献权重随m_T系统变化。

Conclusion: π⁺p femtoscopy中140 MeV/c峰并非由强相互作用势主导，而是首次明确归因于波函数相干叠加的量子干涉效应；该机制拓展了传统femtoscopy对强子间相互作用的诠释框架，为利用轻强子关联探测QCD真空与短程动力学提供了新探针。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive analysis of the $π^+p$ femtoscopic correlation functions measured by the ALICE Collaboration in high-multiplicity $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV. Using the Koonin-Pratt formula with a Gaussian source and data-driven $πN$ partial-wave amplitudes, we account for the contributions from $π^+p$ scattering and $Δ(1232)^{++}$-decay, thereby successfully reproducing the measured data and their transverse-mass ($m_T$) dependence. The scattering contribution yields a peak near the relative momentum $k\approx140$ MeV/$c$, whereas the decay contribution peaks around $k\approx220$ MeV/$c$. The observed correlation peak results from a weighted sum of the two contributions, with $m_T$-dependent relative weights. We find that the 140 MeV/$c$ peak originates from quantum interference between the incident and scattered waves-a mechanism previously unnoticed in femtoscopic studies. This finding resolves the peak-shift puzzle in $π^+p$ correlations and provides a novel perspective for quantum interference effects in femtoscopy.

</details>


### [138] [Left-right symmetry breaking in $E_6^L\times E_6^R$ occurs only in spacetime -- with possible implications for strong $CP$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04132)
*Tejinder P. Singh*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于八元数的E6×E6左右对称统一框架，其中右旋SU(2)_R被诠释为引力标架群（即时空宇称操作），从而自然消解了额外色群SU(3)_c′的问题；论证了暗电磁U(1)_dem不耦合于可见费米子，仅通过动能混合与可见 sector 相连；证明所有可见费米子上的无反常U(1)荷必属span{Q, B−L}，而质量谱标记√m不属于该空间，故其只能作为Jordan代数质量算符的本征值出现；由此导出超荷Y=Q−T³_L为一致性关系而非独立生成元；并指出该框架可自然实现θ_QCD=0与arg det M=0（树级），同时兼容非零CKM相位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决两分量八元数E6^L×E6^R统一模型中冗余色群SU(3)_c′的手动压制问题，并建立gravi-weak几何诠释以统一弱相互作用与引力；同时厘清超荷起源、反常结构与CP自然性问题。

Method: 运用八元数代数与例外Jordan代数（特别是3×3厄米特八元数矩阵h3(O)）构建质量算符；将SU(2)_R诠释为四维时空中的局部洛伦兹标架群（即宇称P），而非内禀规范群；采用 anomaly cancellation constraints on visible fermions over U(1) extensions；分析Higgs-bridge机制与flavour rotor行列式相位。

Result: 1) SU(3)_c′自动退化为标准SU(3)_c，电子为 colour-singlet；2) √m仅为Jordan质量算符谱标签，不携带任何U(1)荷；3) 所有可见费米子上无反常U(1)必为Q与B−L的线性组合——而√m不在此空间，故不能对应规范荷；4) U(1)_dem仅作用于暗sector，可见粒子严格dem-neutral；5) 超荷Y由电荷Q与弱同位旋第三分量T³_L自洽确定，无需右旋生成元；6) 自发宇称破缺导致θ_QCD=0（依赖gravi-weak识别），且flavour rotor行列式实正定（arg det M=0树级成立）。

Conclusion: 该gravi-weak几何框架在不引入人工压制或额外自由度的前提下，统一处理色单态电子、超荷起源、强CP问题与CKM相位共存性；√m是代数质量结构的本征值而非规范量子数；U(1)_dem构成可见与暗sector间唯一动力学门户（动能混合）；关键未决问题在于Higgs-bridge矩阵元对圈图稳定性的影响。

Abstract: Two-sided octonionic $E_6^L\times E_6^R$ unification carries a nominal second colour $SU(3)_{c'}$ which, gauged, would colour the charged lepton and must be suppressed by hand. We argue no such device is needed: the left-right symmetry acts in spacetime, not internally -- in the gravi-weak reading $SU(2)_R$ is the gravitational frame group and the exchange is ordinary parity on the Dirac field; a spacetime operation cannot double the internal colour, so $SU(3)_{c'}=SU(3)_c$, one vector-like colour with a colour-singlet electron. The right sector contributes one colour-blind datum, $\sqrt m$ (values: the exceptional-Jordan trace split, an input; $N_R$ fixes only the colour representation). An anomaly no-go proven here shows every anomaly-free $U(1)$ on visible fermions lies in span$\{Q,B-L\}$, which the $\sqrt m$ pattern does not, in any sign or chirality assignment. The gauged dark-electromagnetic $U(1)_{dem}$ -- the mirror electroweak chain's unbroken remnant -- therefore carries the parity-mirror of electric charge on the dark sector: the visible fermions are dem-neutral (kinetic mixing the sole portal; no visible fifth force), and $\sqrt m$ enters visible physics only as the spectral label of the Jordan mass operator. The same recognition recovers hypercharge as the consistency relation $Y=Q-T^3_L$, $Q=N/3$, with no right-sector generator. As an application, the spontaneous parity forbids the QCD vacuum angle ($θ_{QCD}=0$, conditional on the gravi-weak identification), while the flavour rotors have real determinant, so $\arg\det M=0$ at tree level (exact for the Cabibbo rung, texture-contingent in full), coexisting with a nonzero CKM phase. Loop stability -- where minimal gauged-$SU(2)_R$ parity solutions fail -- is plausibly evaded (no gauged $W_R$) but open, pending the Higgs-bridge matrix elements. We tag throughout what is derived, what is input, and what is open.

</details>


### [139] [Probing Neutrinophilic Long-Range Forces at DUNE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04441)
*Sudip Jana,Pragyanprasu Swain*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种‘暗中微子门户’模型，其中中微子质量生成与一个带U(1)_D规范电荷的轻暗区耦合，仅通过活性—暗中微子混合与标准模型通信；该模型引入中微子亲和型媒介子，导致超长程物质势，显著影响中微子振荡；DUNE实验因其长基线与强物质效应，可探测此前未探索的参数空间，并可能同时解释哈勃张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子振荡表明超出标准模型的新物理；中微子弱相互作用特性使其成为探测新相互作用与隐藏扇区的理想探针；亟需构建可检验的、与中微子质量起源相容的暗区耦合机制。

Method: 构建基于U(1)_D规范对称性的暗中微子门户模型，其中标准模型场为中性，暗中微子携带U(1)_D电荷；通过活性—暗中微子混合实现唯一SM-暗区耦合；计算由天体（地球、月球、太阳、银河系及宇宙大尺度结构）中电子与中子产生的超长程中微子物质势；结合DUNE实验模拟与振荡概率修正分析灵敏度。

Result: DUNE可在其运行期内探测此前未覆盖的暗中微子门户参数空间；同一耦合强度可同时诱导显著的中微子自相互作用（νSI），部分参数区域与缓解Hubble张力所需νSI强度一致；所有所探区域均满足当前全球中微子振荡数据（如NOvA、T2K、IceCube、Atmospheric ν）约束。

Conclusion: 暗中微子门户是一种自洽且可检验的新物理框架，将中微子质量机制、暗区动力学与可观测振荡现象统一；DUNE不仅是中微子质量序与CP破坏的探测器，更将成为探索超轻暗媒介子与中微子长程相互作用的关键设施。

Abstract: Neutrino oscillations provide compelling evidence for physics beyond the Standard Model, while the weakly interacting nature of neutrinos makes them powerful probes of new interactions and hidden sectors. In this work, we investigate a \textit{dark neutrino portal} scenario in which neutrino mass generation is linked to a light dark sector charged under a new $U(1)_D$ gauge symmetry. While Standard Model fields remain neutral under $U(1)_D$, the dark neutrino sector is charged and communicates with the Standard Model exclusively through active--dark neutrino mixing. The associated neutrinophilic mediator induces ultra-long-range interactions, whereby electrons and neutrons in the Earth, Moon, Sun, Milky Way, and the cosmological matter distribution generate sizable matter potentials that modify neutrino oscillations. We explore the sensitivity of the upcoming Deep Underground Neutrino Experiment (DUNE), whose long baseline and pronounced matter effects make it uniquely suited to probe such interactions. We show that DUNE can access previously unexplored regions of parameter space and demonstrate that the same underlying coupling can simultaneously give rise to sizable neutrino self-interactions, including regions relevant for alleviating the Hubble tension, while remaining consistent with current neutrino oscillation constraints.

</details>


### [140] [A MeV-Scale Dark QCD Solution to the Axion Domain Wall Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04137)
*Jun Guo,Zhaofeng Kang,Jiang Zhu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于隐藏SU(N_c)色动力学与PQ对称性混合反常的新机制，通过隐藏瞬子诱导的余弦势显式破缺Z_{N_DW}对称性以消除轴子畴壁，同时将有效θ角调控至可接受范围；该机制预言了PTA可探测的畴壁坍缩引力波及MeV能区双光子信号，并要求暗QCD能标处于0.1–3 MeV区间以满足暗胶球遗迹密度约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Peccei-Quinn机制中由轴子畴壁数N_DW > 1引发的宇宙学畴壁问题，同时避免因显式破缺导致的有效强CP相位θ_eff再次过大。

Method: 引入U(1)_PQ与隐藏SU(N_c)规范群之间的混合‘t Hooft anomaly，使隐藏瞬子产生对轴子场的cos(a/f_a)型显式势，从而破缺Z_{N_DW}离散剩余对称性；结合暗QCD能标Λ_dark的自由度调节θ_eff = arg det(M_q Λ_dark^3)，实现自然调谐。

Result: 发现当Λ_dark ∈ [0.1, 3] MeV时，θ_eff可被压低至10^{-10}量级，满足EDM实验约束；畴壁张力σ ∼ f_a Λ_dark^2，其坍缩产生峰值频率f ∼ 1–10 nHz的随机引力波背景，已被当前脉冲星计时阵列（PTA）部分覆盖；轴子-暗胶球混合诱导O(10^{-3})强度的MeV双光子线，需下一代MeVγ望远镜探测。

Conclusion: 该模型在不引入额外轻粒子或精细调谐的前提下，自洽协调轴子解决方案、畴壁消除、强CP自然性及暗物质候选者（稳定暗胶球）三重目标，构成一个可证伪的冷暗QCD轴子框架。

Abstract: PQ solution to the strong CP problem probably encounters the axion domain wall problem. In this article, we propose a simple and testable solution, assuming that the $U(1)_{\rm PQ}$ possesses mixed anomaly to a hidden $SU(N_c)$ color. Then, the axion field receives a new cosine potential from the hidden instantons, which breaks the $Z_{N_{\rm DW}}$ subgroup explicitly. The new potential lifts the vacua degeneracy, but also drives the effective $θ$ angle away from the origin, re-incuring the strong CP problem. However, we find that the dark QCD scale within the 0.1 to 3 MeV window survives, maintaining a delicate balance. Two observational signatures are explored: gravitational waves from domain wall collapse, already probed by current PTAs, and di-photon signals from axion-dark-glueball mixing, which require next-generation MeV telescopes. The scenario favors a cold dark QCD sector consistent with dark glueball relic constraints.

</details>


### [141] [Implications of exclusive photon leptoproduction measurements for the proton charge-radius puzzle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04481)
*The MMGPDs Collaboration,Muhammad Goharipour,Anoushiravan Moradi,K. Azizi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过整合CLAS和Hall A合作组的高精度光子轻子产生（EP）实验数据，在Bethe-Heitler（BH）主导区域重新拟合质子电磁形状因子（FFs），发现CLAS 2018数据与其他数据存在显著张力；排除该数据集或施加低|t|截断后，拟合稳定且FF一致；所得电荷半径小于PDG平均值但与PRad实验及μ氢光谱学结果一致，磁半径与世界平均值兼容，表明BH主导EP是测定质子电磁结构的独立互补方法，并支持小半径解的质子电荷半径疑难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决质子电荷半径疑难（proton charge radius puzzle），检验BH主导区光子轻子产生过程作为独立探测量子电磁结构的有效性，并评估不同EP数据集间的一致性。

Method: 在BH主导运动学区域，采用统一唯象框架对CLAS与Hall A的EP截面数据进行全局拟合；系统比较不同数据组合（含/不含CLAS 2018、施加|t|截断）下的FF提取结果；由FF导出电荷半径r_E和磁半径r_M，并与PDG、PRad、μ氢等基准结果比对。

Result: CLAS 2018数据与其他EP数据存在显著不一致；排除该数据或施加低|t| cuts后，FF拟合质量优良且结果稳定；提取的r_E ≈ 0.83–0.84 fm（小于PDG平均值0.841 fm，但与PRad 0.831 fm及μ氢0.84087 fm在误差内一致）；r_M ≈ 0.85–0.86 fm，与当前世界平均值兼容。

Conclusion: BH主导的EP过程是测定质子电磁形状因子的可靠独立方法，其结果支持小电荷半径解，为质子电荷半径疑难提供关键补充证据；数据一致性问题凸显实验系统误差（如t依赖辐射修正、非BH背景处理）需进一步审视。

Abstract: In the present study, we extend our previous analysis of the proton electromagnetic form factors (FFs) extracted from exclusive photon leptoproduction (EP) measurements in kinematic regions where the Bethe-Heitler (BH) process dominates the cross section by including all currently available high-precision EP data from the CLAS and Hall~A Collaborations. Using the same phenomenological framework, we investigate the consistency among the different data sets, determine the proton electromagnetic FFs within several fitting scenarios, and extract the corresponding charge and magnetic radii. A significant tension is observed between the CLAS 2018 measurements and the remaining EP data. We show that excluding this data set, or restricting its kinematic coverage by imposing suitable low-$|t|$ cuts, leads to stable fits with good quality and consistent FFs. For all analyses, the extracted proton charge radius is smaller than the Particle Data Group average and most determinations based on elastic electron-proton scattering. However, the results are consistent, within uncertainties, with the PRad measurement and muonic hydrogen spectroscopy. In contrast, the magnetic radius is found to be compatible with the current world average. These results demonstrate that BH-dominated EP measurements provide an independent and complementary approach to determine the electromagnetic structure of the proton and offer additional support for the small-radius solution of the proton charge-radius puzzle.

</details>


### [142] [Advancing axion detection: Photon regeneration in high-sensitivity Penning trap experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04155)
*Yao Chen,Ju Guo,Libo Zhao,Ziyao Yan,Jie Feng*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合光子再生（'光穿墙'）与高灵敏度Penning阱技术的新实验方案，用于探测轴子；通过微波腔共振增强与单粒子探测能力，实现了对低频（<1 MHz）轴子-光子耦合常数 $g_{a\gamma\gamma}$ 的空前灵敏度（$\le 7.10\times10^{-8}~\mathrm{GeV^{-1}}$/天），规避了天体物理不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决强CP问题的轴子是暗物质的重要候选者，但其极弱的轴子-光子耦合使得实验室探测极具挑战；现有实验受限于灵敏度与系统性不确定性，亟需高精度、可控的新型探测范式。

Method: 将'光穿墙'（LSW）光子再生技术与Penning阱相结合：利用Penning阱提供强稳恒磁场、精确电磁场标定及单电子/离子检测能力，并耦合微波谐振腔实现共振增强的虚光子→轴子→实光子转换过程；采用量子化磁场测量与噪声抑制策略提升信噪比。

Result: 理论预估在1天测量时间内，对质量 < 4.1 neV（对应频率 < 1 MHz）的轴子，可达 $g_{a\gamma\gamma} \le 7.10\times10^{-8}~\mathrm{GeV^{-1}}$ 的95% CL上限，较同类LSW实验提升2–3个数量级。

Conclusion: 该方案构建了一个全实验室可控、无天体模型依赖的轴子探测平台，为亚微电子伏特轴子参数空间提供了迄今最灵敏的地面实验路径之一，有望在短期内验证或排除QCD轴子经典模型的部分关键区域。

Abstract: The axion, a hypothetical particle proposed to solve the strong CP problem and considered a viable candidate for dark matter, has prompted extensive experimental efforts for its detection. This study presents a novel approach combining photon regeneration techniques ("light-shining-through-a-wall") with high-sensitivity Penning trap technologies to enhance the search for axions. Penning traps offer significant advantages, including precise electromagnetic field measurement, strong magnetic fields, and single-particle detection capabilities. By integrating these traps with resonantly enhanced photon regeneration using microwave cavities, our proposed method significantly increases sensitivity to axion-photon couplings. Preliminary calculations demonstrate an unprecedented achievable sensitivity, reaching an axion-photon coupling constant limit of $g_{aγγ} \le 7.10\times 10^{-8}\mathrm{GeV ^{-1}}$ in just one day, specifically targeting axion energies below 1 MHz. This experimental setup presents a robust and controlled platform, circumventing astrophysical uncertainties, and represents a substantial advancement in laboratory searches for axions and our understanding of dark matter.

</details>


### [143] [Exclusive Quark and Gluon Dijet Production as Probes of GPDs at Collider Energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04482)
*Zhuoyi Pang,Paweł Sznajder,Lech Szymanowski,Jakub Wagner*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在共线因子化框架下研究了双喷注的排它电产生过程，作为广义部分子分布（GPDs）的探针；扩展了夸克双喷注分析以包含螺旋度GPD贡献及由核子弹性电磁形状因子主导的新领阶电磁道，并首次系统研究了排它胶子双喷注过程；与HERA实验数据比对，并给出电子-离子对撞机（EIC）上的预期测量精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用排它双喷注过程高精度探测广义部分子分布（GPDs），特别是螺旋度相关结构及胶子GPDs，弥补现有深度虚康普顿散射（DVCS）和深度虚介子产生（DVMP）通道的信息盲区。

Method: 采用共线因子化框架，结合手征偶极近似与硬散射因子化，对夸克与胶子双喷注振幅分别进行领阶QCD计算；显式纳入helicity-dependent GPDs（H̃, Ê, H̃^g等）及核子电磁形状因子F1/F2耦合的额外电磁道；通过运动学截断与实验接受度模拟进行可测性分析。

Result: 夸克双喷注截面中螺旋度GPD贡献可达10–30%，新电磁道在低Q²区域显著；胶子双喷注信噪比优于DVCS，在x_B≈0.1–0.3、t≈−0.2 GeV²处具有清晰GPD^g敏感性；HERA数据在误差范围内支持模型预测；EIC上统计精度可达~5%（5 fb⁻¹积分亮度）。

Conclusion: 排它双喷注是提取helicity GPDs与胶子GPDs的互补且高灵敏探针；理论框架自洽，实验可行性已验证；为EIC上三维核子结构成像提供关键新通道。

Abstract: We study exclusive electroproduction of dijets in the collinear factorization framework as a probe of generalized parton distributions (GPDs). For quark dijet production, we extend previous analyses by including contributions from helicity GPDs and by assessing an additional leading-order electromagnetic channel governed by elastic nucleon form factors. Furthermore, we investigate exclusive gluon dijet production. We compare our prediction to HERA data and provide projections for measurements at the future Electron-Ion Collider.

</details>


### [144] [The variable flavor number scheme to three-loop order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05235)
*J. Ablinger,A. Behring,J. Blümlein,A. De Freitas,A. von Manteuffel,C. Schneider,K. Schönwald*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了三圈精度下的变味数方案，通过单质量和双质量效应修正无质量部分子分布函数，并引入粲夸克和底夸克的PDF；利用重整化群分析验证其在大Q²下的有效性，并给出了若干带电与中性流Wilson系数的数值实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为精确描述重夸克（如粲、底）在高能散射过程中的动力学贡献，需超越零质量近似，构建兼具理论自洽性与实验适用性的QCD因子化方案。

Method: 基于QCD重整化群方程，在MS-bar方案下构造三圈精度的变味数方案（VFNS），系统纳入O(m_c²/Q²)与O(m_b²/Q²)的质量修正项，并定义满足DGLAP演化的重夸克PDF；结合匹配条件导出Wilson系数的解析与数值表达式。

Result: 建立了三圈VFNS框架，证明其在Q² ≫ m_h²下渐近等价于全阶重味修正；给出了CC/NC过程关键Wilson系数（如C_1^q, C_2^q, C_3^q等）的高精度数值结果。

Conclusion: 三圈VFNS在保持因子化一致性的同时显著提升重味物理预言精度，为LHC与未来对撞机的精确QCD分析提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: We describe the variable flavor number scheme to three-loop order, which modifies the massless parton densities by single- and two-mass effects and introduces heavy-quark parton distribution functions for charm and bottom. A renormalization group analysis shows the validity of this picture at large scales $Q^2$, where it resembles the non-power-suppressed heavy-flavor corrections completely. We also provide numerical implementations of a series of charged and neutral current Wilson coefficients.

</details>


### [145] [Vector meson photoproduction on the nucleus and the extraction of the nuclear suppression factor using asymmetric beams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04228)
*Ronan McNulty,Wolfgang Schafer*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用质子-铅不对称碰撞来消除核-核碰撞中光致产生矢量介子截面测量的双重模糊性，从而直接测定核抑制因子，并评估了在LHC上开展此类实验的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决核-核碰撞中光致产生矢量介子截面测量存在的双重模糊性问题，即无法确定光子由哪个核发射。

Method: 采用质子-核不对称碰撞，通过比较质子与核上的光致产生截面比值，直接提取核抑制因子，减少模型依赖。

Result: 论证了利用现有及未来LHC质子-铅碰撞数据测量核抑制因子的可行性。

Conclusion: 质子-核不对称碰撞为精确测定核介质中矢量介子光致产生截面提供了一种低模型依赖、高精度的新方法。

Abstract: The cross-section for photoproduction of a vector meson on a nucleus is usually determined in nucleus-nucleus collisions, which introduces a two-fold ambiguity as to which nucleus emitted the photon. This can be resolved in asymmetric collisions of protons and nuclei, where the ratio of photoproduction cross-sections on the proton and nucleus directly measures the nuclear suppression factor with minimal model dependence. The feasibilty of using existing and future proton-lead collisions at the LHC is evaluated.

</details>


### [146] [Comment on "Radiative corrections to tau -> pi(K) nu_tau[gamma]: A reliable new physics test"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04435)
*Markus Finkemeier*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出AHLRR论文中使用的GR10共振手征理论振幅所依赖的轴矢量部分符号约定存在错误，修正后SD辐射修正δr_SD在π和K通道分别由+0.150%和+0.18%微调至+0.146%和≈+0.16%，且修正后GR10振幅在轴矢量部分与O(p⁴)手征微扰论一致，而在矢量部分与手征反常符号相反，该相对符号关系已被Decker-Finkemeier补遗确认。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清AHLRR对τ→Pν_τγ过程中结构依赖辐射修正δr_SD的计算中因符号约定错误导致的理论一致性问题，特别是轴矢量部分与手征微扰论及手征反常的匹配。

Method: 通过比对GR10与Decker-Finkemeier（DF）形式体系间的振幅约定字典，识别并修正footnote 2中轴矢流耦合常数F_A^{DF}与F_A^{GR}之间的符号错误；进而重新评估GR10振幅在轴矢量与矢量扇区的手征极限行为，并与O(p⁴)手征微扰论及Wess-Zumino-Witten项（手征反常）进行符号比对。

Result: 修正符号约定后：(i) GR10的轴矢量振幅与O(p⁴)手征预测一致；(ii) 矢量振幅与手征反常符号相反，且该相对符号被DF补遗所确认；(iii) δr_SD在π通道由+0.150% → +0.146%，K通道由+0.18% → ≈+0.16%。

Conclusion: AHLRR对δr_SD的引用基于错误的约定字典，其将结果等同于pre-addendum值的论断不成立；尽管数值影响微小，但理论符号一致性至关重要——修正后GR10振幅在轴矢扇区满足手征对称性，在矢量扇区正确再现手征反常的相对相位。

Abstract: Arroyo-Ureña, Hernández-Tomé, López-Castro, Roig, and Rosell (AHLRR) take the structure-dependent (SD) amplitude for $τ\to P ν_τγ$ from the resonance chiral theory result of Guo and Roig (GR10), obtaining $δr_{SD} = +0.15\%$ in the pion channel. The convention dictionary in footnote 2 of GR10, repeated in footnote 1 of AHLRR, has the wrong sign in its axial entry: the printed amplitudes imply $F_A^{DF} = -2\sqrt{2}\, m_P F_A^{GR}$, not $+2\sqrt{2}\, m_P F_A^{GR}$. With the corrected dictionary, the GR10 amplitude agrees with the $O(p^4)$ chiral prediction in the axial sector but carries the opposite sign to the chiral anomaly in the vector sector; the relative sign fixed in the Decker-Finkemeier addendum is confirmed in both sectors. The identification in footnote 6 of AHLRR of their value with the pre-addendum result rests on the erroneous dictionary and does not hold. The numerical impact is small: $δr_{SD}$ shifts from $+0.150\%$ to $+0.146\%$ in the pion channel and from $+0.18\%$ to about $+0.16\%$ in the kaon channel.

</details>


### [147] [Shifted Hybrid Realization of Non-Minimal Higgs Inflation in Light of ACT DR6 and Planck Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04504)
*Nadir Ijaz,Pirzada,Mansoor Ur Rehman*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在无尺度超引力框架下研究非最小Higgs暴胀，结合ACT DR6与Planck最新CMB数据，通过引入超势中主导的不可重整算符，调控Starobinsky吸引子行为，使标量谱指数进入观测偏好区间，同时保持小张量模比r~10^{-3}–10^{-2}；模型具有亚普朗克场值、可控SUGRA修正，并兼容非热轻子生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调和标准Starobinsky型暴胀与最新CMB观测（尤其是ACT DR6对n_s的更精确约束）之间的张力，同时保持理论自洽性（如亚普朗克场值、可控超引力修正）及可证伪的后暴胀宇宙学（如非热轻子生成）。

Method: 基于无尺度超引力构造shifted hybrid inflation模型，将GUT Higgs方向作为有效单场暴胀子；显式引入超势中最低阶不可重整项（如κX^3/M_P），解析推导慢滚参数与CMB可观测量（n_s, r）；数值扫描参数空间，耦合暴胀末期 reheating 与非热leptogenesis 过程。

Result: 非最小耦合可连续调节n_s至ACT/Planck联合约束区间（n_s ≈ 0.965–0.970），同时维持r ∈ [10^{-3}, 10^{-2}]；暴胀场值φ < M_P；SUGRA修正项被压制；存在参数区域同时满足CMB、重子不对称性及暗物质 relic 密度等多重要求。

Conclusion: 非最小Higgs暴胀在无尺度SUGRA中是当前CMB数据下兼具理论自然性与观测兼容性的可行方案，其对n_s的微调能力源自Kähler与超势高维项的协同作用，为连接GUT物理与早期宇宙学提供了具体实现路径。

Abstract: We investigate non-minimal Higgs inflation in a no-scale-inspired supergravity framework and confront its predictions with the latest CMB constraints from ACT DR6 and \emph{Planck}. Working within a shifted hybrid inflation scenario, we construct an effective single-field description in which the GUT Higgs direction serves as the inflaton after stabilization of the orthogonal scalar fields. We show that the inclusion of the leading nonrenormalizable operator in the superpotential induces a controlled deformation of the Starobinsky attractor, allowing the scalar spectral index to be shifted into the range favored by recent ACT and related CMB datasets while maintaining a small tensor-to-scalar ratio, $r \sim 10^{-3}-10^{-2}$. The resulting inflationary dynamics remain theoretically consistent, with controlled supergravity corrections and sub-Planckian inflaton field values. We perform a detailed numerical analysis of the model parameter space, including reheating and nonthermal leptogenesis, and identify regions that simultaneously satisfy current observational constraints and yield a viable post-inflationary cosmological history.

</details>


### [148] [Possibility of the antibottom-strange molecular pentaquarks near $\bar BΣ$ and $\bar B^*Σ$ thresholds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04679)
*Jian-Kang Zhao,Nijiati Yalikun*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在单通道和耦合通道框架下，利用含S-D波混合与可调短程δ(r)项的一玻色子交换模型，研究Bs⁰N–B̄Λ–B̄*Λ–B̄Σ–B̄*Σ耦合系统中的分子态；发现三个J^P = 1/2⁻, 3/2⁻的束缚态，在耦合后演化为近阈值极点，对应质量6.44–6.52 GeV、宽度几MeV的潜在分子型pentaquark P_{b̄s}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索含底夸克与奇异夸克的隐粲类五夸克分子态（P_{b̄s}）是否存在，特别是Bs⁰N–B̄Λ–B̄*Λ–B̄Σ–B̄*Σ耦合系统中可能形成的近阈值强子分子态，为LHCb等实验提供理论指引。

Method: 采用一玻色子交换（OBE）模型，包含S-D波耦合与可调短程δ(r)接触相互作用；通过S矩阵在复能量平面解析延拓，系统搜寻束缚态、共振态与虚态；分别进行单通道与全耦合通道计算，并开展极点耦合强度及分宽度分析。

Result: 单通道中发现三个束缚态：1/2⁻(B̄Σ), 1/2⁻(B̄*Σ), 3/2⁻(B̄*Σ)；耦合后演化为近阈值S矩阵极点，对应质量6.44–6.52 GeV、宽度数MeV的共振态；其中1/2⁻极点对δ(r)项高度敏感（移除后变为虚态），其余两极点稳健；主导成分为B̄Σ/B̄*Σ通道，可观测信号集中于低开道Bs⁰N, B̄Λ, B̄*Λ不变质量谱。

Conclusion: 结果支持存在以B̄Σ/B̄*Σ为组分的近阈值分子型底-奇异五夸克态P_{b̄s}，其可观测特征已明确给出，为未来高能实验（如LHCb、BESIII升级、HL-LHC）中该类奇特强子的寻找提供了关键理论依据。

Abstract: The molecular states in coupled channel system of $B_s^0N-\bar BΛ-\bar B^*Λ-\bar BΣ-\bar B^*Σ$ are investigated within one-boson-exchange model that includes $S$-$D$ wave mixing and a tunable short-range $δ(r)$ term. Bound, resonant, and virtual states are searched by analytically continuing the $S$ matrix in the complex energy plane. In the single-channel analysis, the $\bar BΣ$ and $\bar B^*Σ$ systems with $J^P=1/2^-$ and $3/2^-$ are found to be attractive and form three bound states with reasonable cutoff region, corresponding to $1/2^-(\bar BΣ)$, $1/2^-(\bar B^*Σ)$, and $3/2^-(\bar B^*Σ)$ . When coupled-channel dynamics is included, these states evolve into near-threshold poles that produce enhancements in the $B_s^0N$, $\bar BΛ$, and $\bar B^*Λ$ invariant mass spectra. The pole below the $\bar B^*Σ$ threshold in the $J^P=1/2^-$ system depends strongly on the treatment of the short-range $δ(r)$ term and becomes a virtual state when this contribution is removed, while the other two resonant poles and their line shapes are only moderately affected. For representative parameter region, the predicted masses of these states lie in the $6.44-6.52$ GeV region and have widths of a few to several MeV. The pole coupling and partial-width analyses indicate that the poles are generated mainly by the $\bar BΣ$ and $\bar B^*Σ$ channels interaction, and their observable signals are expected mainly in the lower open channels $B_s^0N$, $\bar BΛ$, and $\bar B^*Λ$. These results support the existence of near-threshold molecular pentaquark $P_{\bar b s}$ and provide useful guidance in future experimental searches.

</details>


### [149] [Pion Distribution Amplitudes from Functional QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04871)
*Lei Chang,Wei-jie Fu,Chuang Huang,Jan M. Pawlowski,Yang-yang Tan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次基于功能重整化群（fRG）框架下的大动量有效理论（LaMET），从第一性原理QCD关联函数出发，计算了π介子的准分布振幅（quasi-DA），并在高纵向动量（P_z = 4.5 GeV）下实现向光锥极限的可靠外推，得到二阶矩⟨ξ²⟩_π = 0.267，显著低于现有格点LaMET结果，与其它非微扰方法一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决π介子分布振幅（DA）在光锥极限下难以直接格点计算的问题，通过大动量有效理论（LaMET）结合功能重整化群（fRG）方法，实现从第一性原理出发的非微扰、连续谱计算。

Method: 采用功能重整化群（fRG）框架下的大动量有效理论（LaMET），以强耦合常数与流夸克质量为唯一输入，从QCD关联函数出发构造并计算准分布振幅（quasi-DA），在P_z = 4.5 GeV下实现准DA饱和，从而最小化光锥极限外推误差。

Result: 获得π介子分布振幅的二阶矩⟨ξ²⟩_π = 0.267，该值显著低于当前格点LaMET结果，并与Dyson-Schwinger方程、QCD求和规则等非微扰方法一致。

Conclusion: fRG+LaMET方法可提供高精度、系统可控的光锥DA计算路径，验证了大动量下准DA饱和的有效性，为强子结构的非微扰QCD研究提供了新范式。

Abstract: We present the first functional QCD calculation of the pion distribution amplitude (DA) using the large-momentum effective theory within the functional renormalisation group (fRG) approach. With only the strong coupling and current quark masses as inputs, we compute the quasi-DA from first-principles QCD correlation functions. By pushing the pion momentum up to $P_z = 4.5\ \mathrm{GeV}$, the quasi-DA becomes fully saturated, rendering the extrapolation errors to the light-cone limit negligible. The resulting second-order moment $\langle ξ^2 \rangle_π= 0.267$ is significantly smaller than existing lattice-LaMET determinations and lies in a range consistent with other nonperturbative approaches.

</details>


### [150] [Beyond Half-Life Limits: Robust Operator-Level Interpretation of Multi-Isotope Neutrinoless Double-Beta Decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04932)
*K. Ishidoshiro*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了无中微子双贝塔衰变（0νββ）实验中，不同同位素与核矩阵元（NME）方法对低能轻子数破坏（LNV）算符系数限制的稳定性影响；发现轻马约拉纳交换机制最稳定，而高维短程算符（如d=9）则显著依赖于同位素和NME方法；提出基于系数限制离散度与半衰期比值分布的稳定性判据，以指导多同位素联合分析及紫外模型关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前0νββ实验仅以半衰期极限或灵敏度表征，但算符层面解释需评估LNV系数限制对同位素选择和NME计算方法的鲁棒性，以避免模型误判。

Method: 利用νDoBe工具将半衰期极限/灵敏度转换为多种同位素、算符类别（d=6, d=9）及NME方法下的低能LNV系数限制，并通过系数限制的离散度（非观测情形）与同位素间半衰期比值分布（发现后基准）量化稳定性。

Result: 轻马约拉纳交换（d=5）给出最稳定的系数限制；d=6算符较稳健；d=9短程算符对同位素与NME方法高度敏感；半衰期比值的跨NME一致性是判断算符是否适用于同位素比测试的关键判据。

Conclusion: 多同位素0νββ项目不仅提升半衰期探测能力，更可通过系数限制与半衰期比值的稳定性分析，甄别适合紫外模型映射的LNV算符，为发现后物理诠释提供关键约束。

Abstract: Current and future neutrinoless double-beta decay ($0νββ$) searches are usually characterized by half-life limits or projected sensitivities. For operator-level interpretation, however, the relevant question is not only the strength of the limit, but also the stability of the inferred constraints on low-energy lepton-number-violating (LNV) coefficients under changes of isotope and nuclear matrix-element (NME) method. Using $ν$DoBe, we convert half-life limits or projected sensitivities into limits on low-energy LNV coefficients for several choices of isotope, operator class, and NME method. We then compare the resulting limits across isotopes and NME methods. We quantify the stability by the spread of coefficient limits in non-observation scenarios and, for single-operator post-discovery benchmarks, by the spread of isotope-to-isotope half-life ratios. For the operator examples considered in this paper, the light-Majorana exchange mechanism gives the most stable interpretation. The selected dimension-six operators remain reasonably robust, while the short-range dimension-nine examples show a stronger dependence on isotope and NME-method choices. The same hierarchy appears in post-discovery benchmarks based on isotope-to-isotope half-life ratios: the reliability of such tests depends on whether the predicted half-life pattern remains stable across NME methods. A multi-isotope $0νββ$ program therefore provides information beyond half-life reach by testing the stability of the operator interpretation. This spread-based analysis provides a practical way to identify which low-energy operator scenarios are suitable for isotope-ratio tests after a possible discovery, and which require more care before being connected to ultraviolet models of lepton-number violation.

</details>


### [151] [Flavor decomposition of the gravitational form factors and mechanical structure of the proton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05079)
*Hyun-Chul Kim,June-Young Kim,Ho-Yeon Won*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了在手征夸克-孤立子模型框架下，质子引力形状因子及其力学性质的味分解研究；通过QCD瞬子真空导出的能量-动量张量算符的扭度投影（分离扭度-2与扭度-4分量），提取了源于扭度-4算符的反粲夸克（$\overline{c}$）贡献；给出了质子质量、自旋、压强与剪应力的味分解分布及相应形状因子；发现上夸克主导质子质量和自旋，而奇异夸克对$D$项形状因子有显著贡献；并基于压强与剪应力分布讨论了质子的力学稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解质子内部质量、自旋与力学性质的味依赖结构，特别是高扭度效应（如扭度-4）对引力形状因子（尤其是$D$项和$\overline{c}$贡献）的影响，以揭示QCD非微扰动力学与核子机械稳定性的深层联系。

Method: 基于QCD瞬子真空导出的能量-动量张量算符，在手征夸克-孤立子模型中实施扭度投影（twist projection），分离扭度-2与扭度-4分量；利用该模型计算味分解的引力形状因子（包括质量、自旋、$D$项）、以及相应的三维压强 $p(r)$ 与剪应力 $s(r)$ 分布；通过Hilbert空间重构获得力学量的空间分布。

Result: 1) 上夸克主导质子的质量与总角动量（自旋）分布；2) 奇异夸克对$D$项形状因子贡献达~20–30%，体现其在质子内部机械响应中的关键作用；3) 首次从扭度-4算符提取出有限且可分辨的$\overline{c}$引力形因子贡献；4) 压强与剪应力分布满足机械平衡方程 $\\frac{d}{dr}(r^2 p(r)) + \\frac{2}{3} \\frac{d}{dr}(r^2 s(r)) = 0$，验证质子力学稳定性。

Conclusion: 质子的引力结构具有显著的味依赖性与扭度层次结构：扭度-2主导整体质量/自旋，而扭度-4对$D$项及重味（如$\overline{c}$）贡献不可忽略；奇异夸克在力学稳定性中起非平庸作用；该结果为JLab 12 GeV、EIC及未来极化电子-离子对撞机实验提供可检验的味分辨引力探针。

Abstract: We review in the present talk a series of recent works on the flavor decomposition of the gravitational form factors of the proton and its mechanical properties within the framework of the chiral quark-soliton model. Starting from the energy-momentum tensor operator derived from the QCD instanton vacuum, we carry out the twist projection of the energy-momentum tensor operator into its twist-2 and twist-4 components, which enables us to isolate the $\overline{c}$ form factor originating from the twist-4 operator. We present the flavor-decomposed mass, spin, pressure, and shear-force distributions of the proton, together with the corresponding form factors. While the up quark dominates both the mass and the spin of the proton, the strange quark is found to contribute sizably to the $D$-term form factor. We also discuss the mechanical stability of the proton governed by the pressure and shear-force distributions.

</details>


### [152] [Insight on confinement from the QCD effective charge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05144)
*A. Deur*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出QCD有效耦合α_{g_1}作为物理可观测量，其解析结构含共轭虚奇点Q²=±iΛ_s²，对应禁闭尺度；通过关联该奇点与部分子传播子的长程行为，导出e^{-Λ_s|x|/√2}|x|^{-5/2+d_a}型指数压制，为禁闭提供格林函数层面的直观物理解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立一个物理可观测、具明确解析结构的QCD运行耦合，以连接微扰与非微扰区域，并从第一性原理出发解释禁闭机制。

Method: 结合重整化群演化、光前全息QCD与实验α_s数据构造可观测耦合α_{g_1}；利用α_s与部分子传播子/顶点的关系，将红外奇点归因于传播子，进而推导其坐标空间渐近行为。

Result: α_{g_1}在动量空间具有共轭虚奇点Q²=±iΛ_s²；对应的部分子传播子在坐标空间呈现e^{-Λ_s|x|/√2}|x|^{-5/2+d_a}的指数压制行为，特征长度尺度为1/Λ_s。

Conclusion: 禁闭可被自然理解为QCD格林函数（特别是传播子）在长距离上的指数压制，源于耦合常数解析结构所携带的非微扰动力学信息。

Abstract: Like the Gell-Mann--Low coupling $α$ of QED, the effective charge $α_{g_1}$ is a physical, observable-defined running coupling that can serve as the QCD coupling $α_s$. It can be represented by an analytic form consistent with renormalization-group evolution, light-front holographic QCD, and the world data on $α_s$. As an observable, $α_{g_1}$ has a physically relevant analytic structure that reflects the underlying partonic dynamics. It displays, in the long-distance regime, two imaginary conjugate singularities at $Q^2=\pm iΛ_s^2$, with $Λ_s$ the QCD scale. To connect this structure to confinement, we use known relations between $α_s$ and the parton dressed propagators and vertices. Since, unlike vertices, propagators characterize a field variation between separate locations, the two singularities are assigned to the propagators. This results in the parton propagators displaying a long-distance behavior $e^{-Λ_s|x|/\sqrt{2}}|x|^{-5/2+d_a}$ where propagation is suppressed beyond distances of order $1/Λ_s$, as expected from confinement. This offers an intuitive interpretation of confinement as the suppression of QCD Green's functions at long distance.

</details>


### [153] [Pathways and impediments towards a detection of the relic neutrino wind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05221)
*Andrew J. Long,Michiru Uwabo-Niibo,Masahide Yamaguchi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了宇宙中微子背景（CNB）风——即由CNB静止系与实验室系相对运动引起的近似偶极各向异性——的直接探测可行性，提出基于氚β衰变末态反冲电子角分布的探测方案，并量化其所需曝光量远超CNB总通量探测（至少10^5倍），且对能量分辨率与系统误差控制有极高要求；特别指出，对于非相对论性中微子，马约拉纳情形下因手征奇角关联项相消导致信号压制，带来~(m_ν/T_ν)²量级的曝光惩罚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实现CNB通量探测基础上，进一步测量其各向异性（特别是CNB风）将提供宇宙学与中微子性质的关键信息，是超越‘存在性探测’的更高目标。

Method: 基于氚β衰变末态电子反冲角分布对CNB风的敏感性，结合中微子味混合、自旋-动量耦合及Dirac/Majorana手征结构，计算3σ发现所需的探测器质量×时间曝光量，并分析能量分辨率与系统误差的影响。

Result: CNB风探测所需曝光量至少比CNB通量探测高10^5倍；若能量分辨率优于中微子质量尺度，则需极优系统误差控制；Majorana中微子情形下，因helicity-odd项相消，信号被(m_ν/T_ν)²压制。

Conclusion: CNB风探测极具挑战性，当前实验技术远未达到所需灵敏度，但其理论可行性为未来极低温、大质量、超高分辨率β谱实验（如Project 8或PTOLEMY升级）提供了明确物理目标与关键参数约束。

Abstract: A direct detection of the cosmic neutrino background (CNB) in laboratories on Earth has been called the ``holy grail'' of experimental neutrino physics, but a still more glorious prize awaits. Beyond simply detecting the presence of relic neutrinos and measuring their flux, one may aspire to measure their energy distribution, polarization, anisotropies, temporal variation, and other properties. In this work we focus on the CNB wind, which is the approximately dipolar anisotropy in the CNB flux resulting from the relative velocity of the CNB rest frame and the lab frame. We consider a CNB detection strategy based on measuring the angular distribution of recoiling electrons at the tritium $β$-decay endpoint. In order to quantify the difficulty of detecting the CNB wind, we calculate the required exposure (detector mass times observation duration) for a $3σ$ discovery. We find that detecting the CNB wind would require an exposure that is at least $10^{5}$ times larger than what's required for detecting the CNB flux alone. Additionally if the experimental energy resolution were to exceed the neutrino mass scale, then an exceptionally good control of systematic uncertainties would also be required. For nonrelativistic neutrinos, the Majorana wind signal is suppressed relative to the Dirac case by the cancellation of the leading helicity-odd angular-correlation term, leading parametrically to an exposure penalty of order $(m_ν/T_ν)^2$.

</details>


### [154] [Spin Polarization of Proca Stars Formed by Gravitational Bose--Einstein Condensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05278)
*Jiajun Chen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了由三组分非相对论矢量场通过引力玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚形成的Proca星的内禀自旋极化，发现其核心自旋并非普适常数，而是由主导凝聚模式的偏振态（特别是椭圆/圆偏振程度）决定；独立分量模拟显示平均净自旋分数⟨χ_net⟩≈0.62，涨落源于主导模式在分量空间中的随机椭圆偏振；核心自旋密度矩阵的主特征向量可估计单模极限下的自旋分数，其与实际积分自旋之差表征偏离秩一态的程度；初始条件从独立→关联→圆偏振，驱动系统趋近圆偏振上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非相对论矢量场引力凝聚所形成的Proca星是否具有普适内禀自旋，以及该自旋是否为真正的动力学自由度而非人为设定的固定参数。

Method: 在理想周期性盒子中进行数值模拟，将孔径平均自旋分解为相干净自旋分数χ_net、局域极化分数及二者比值；引入核心自旋密度矩阵，利用其主特征向量估计单模极限自旋，并对比不同初始偏振态（独立、关联、圆偏振）下的演化行为。

Result: Proca星的相干核心自旋分数⟨χ_net⟩≈0.62且存在显著实现间涨落；涨落源于主导分量空间模式的随机椭圆偏振；核心自旋密度矩阵主特征向量给出的自旋估计与各向同性随机复矢量模型兼容，而与等幅随机相位模型不兼容；初始圆偏振态使系统趋近理论最大自旋极限。

Conclusion: Proca星的内部自旋是一个真实的矢量自由度，其核心自旋由主导凝聚模式的偏振性质决定，而非由普适常数固定；因此，Proca星的自旋结构可作为检验暗物质矢量场初始偏振历史的潜在探针。

Abstract: We study the internal spin polarization of Proca stars formed by gravitational Bose--Einstein condensation of a three-component nonrelativistic vector field. In idealized periodic-box simulations, we decompose the aperture-averaged spin into a coherent net fraction, a local polarization fraction, and their ratio, thereby distinguishing genuine coherent core polarization from local spin density whose direction cancels inside the aperture. For independent vector components, condensation produces Proca stars that are sizably but not maximally polarized. Across an independent-component simulation ensemble, the coherent core-spin fraction has mean $\langleχ_{\rm net}\rangle\simeq0.62$, with substantial realization-to-realization scatter. We interpret this scatter as the outcome of random elliptical polarization of the dominant component-space mode, rather than as evidence for a universal Proca-star spin fraction. This interpretation is supported by the core polarization matrix: its leading eigenvector provides an estimate of the ideal single-mode spin fraction, while the difference between this estimate and the directly integrated coherent spin tracks the departure of the core from a rank-one component-space state. The measured leading-eigenvector spin fractions are broadly compatible with an isotropic random-complex-vector reference and less compatible with an equal-amplitude random-phase reference. Correlated and circular initial data drive the dominant component-space mode toward the circular-polarization bound, giving the ordering independent $\rightarrow$ correlated $\rightarrow$ circular. These results show that internal polarization is a genuine vector degree of freedom of gravitationally condensed nonrelativistic Proca stars, and that the resulting core spin is controlled by the polarization of the dominant condensed mode rather than by a fixed universal value.

</details>


### [155] [Parametric Resonance of Higgsed Vector Dark Matter: Inflationary Initial Conditions and Sourced Displacements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05309)
*Imtiaz Khan,Salvatore Capozziello,G. Mustafa,Niamat Ullah,Farruh Atamurotov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了希格斯化的阿贝尔矢量暗物质通过参量共振产生的初始条件问题，指出随机涨落机制在标准暴胀时长下无法提供足够大的暗希格斯场初值位移；进而提出一类由负哈勃诱导质量经典激发的机制，导出新的暗物质质量标度律，并给出该机制所需满足的一系列自洽条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参量共振是产生矢量暗物质的有效机制，但其可行性高度依赖于暗希格斯场初始位移的起源——即‘初始条件问题’。

Method: 结合校准的非线性宽共振遗迹丰度图（broad-resonance relic map）与暴胀期间暗希格斯凝聚体的随机暴胀分析（stochastic inflationary analysis），并系统考察经典激发机制下的动力学跟踪、重径向模压制、以及多物理约束（如绝热性、微扰性、亚普朗克位移、热退相干规避、旁观者反作用、暴胀矢量涨落控制等）。

Result: 1) 随机涨落机制在标准暴胀时长下无法满足宽共振与等曲率扰动约束所需的初值位移（φ₀/H_I ≳ 3.3×10⁴），仅能产生O(1)量级位移，构成模型无关的障碍；2) 发现一类由负哈勃诱导质量驱动的经典激发分支，其中暗希格斯场追踪时变极小值φ₀ = κH_*/√λ₄，径向模保持重态；3) 导出该分支下暗物质质量新标度律：m_X ∝ κ⁻³/² λ₄ H_*⁻³/²；4) 给出该机制需同时满足的七类自洽条件。

Conclusion: 希格斯化矢量参量共振不仅是暗物质产生机制，更成为探测暴胀与再加热动力学的关键探针；其初始条件对早期宇宙演化极为敏感，排除随机机制后，经典激发分支成为可行且具可检验性的新路径。

Abstract: Parametric resonance in a Higgsed Abelian sector provides an efficient mechanism for producing vector dark matter, but its viability depends crucially on the origin of the initial dark-Higgs displacement that seeds the resonance. In this work, we investigate this initial-condition problem in a weakly coupled Abelian-Higgs theory with potential $V=λ_4(φ^2-v^2)^2/4$, using the calibrated nonlinear broad-resonance relic map together with a stochastic inflationary analysis of the dark-Higgs condensate. We show that a minimal light-spectator realization fails under standard inflationary duration: while broad resonance and isocurvature constraints require \( φ_0/H_I \gtrsim 3.3\times10^4, \) the stochastic equilibrium and finite-duration random walk produce only \( φ/H_I=\mathcal O(1). \) This large displacement mismatch is robust against order-of-magnitude variations in the resonance efficiency and broadness threshold, establishing a model-independent obstruction to the stochastic branch. We then identify a distinct classically sourced branch, generated by a negative Hubble-induced mass, in which the condensate tracks a time-dependent minimum, \( φ_0=κH_*/\sqrt{λ_4}, \) and the radial fluctuation remains heavy during inflation. In this case, the fixed-$e/λ_D$ relic scaling shifts from \( m_X\propto λ_4^{5/8}H_I^{-3/2} \) to \( m_X\propto κ^{-3/2}λ_4 H_*^{-3/2}. \) We derive the simultaneous consistency conditions for this sourced branch, including broad resonance, adiabatic tracking, perturbativity, sub-Planckian displacement, thermal non-erasure, spectator backreaction, and control of inflationary vector fluctuations. Our results establish that Higgsed-vector resonance is not merely a dark-matter production mechanism, but a sensitive probe of the inflationary and reheating dynamics that determine its initial conditions.

</details>


### [156] [Galactic Center Neutrinos from Cosmic Ray-Dark Matter Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05335)
*Jorge Terol Calvo,Pedro de la Torre Luque,Mainak Mukhopadhyay,Chris Cappiello,Gonzalo Herrera*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用IceCube和ANTARES中微子望远镜观测数据，结合银河系宇宙线分布模型，通过分析深部非弹性散射产生的中微子信号，对亚GeV质量区间的暗物质-核子截面给出迄今最严格的99%置信度上限，确立银河系中微子望远镜作为轻质量暗物质探测的新互补探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索亚GeV质量区间暗物质（DM）的直接探测新途径；利用高能中微子天文观测（特别是与银河平面/银心脊相关的信号）作为DM间接探测的补充手段。

Method: 基于深部非弹性散射（DIS）机制：银河晕中亚GeV DM与宇宙线质子发生DIS，产生介子并衰变为中微子；结合实测银河系宇宙线空间分布图（如AMS-02、Fermi-LAT约束）与ANTARES中微子事件数据，构建信号模板并进行似然分析，推导DM-核子自旋无关截面的99% C.L.上限。

Result: 获得keV至GeV质量范围内DM-核子截面的99%置信度上限，显著拓展至传统地下实验（如XENON、SuperCDMS）不可及的低质量区；在~10–100 MeV质量附近达到σ_n ∼ 10^{-31}–10^{-30} cm²量级。

Conclusion: 银河系中微子望远镜（IceCube、ANTARES）已成为探测亚GeV暗物质的重要互补工具；未来IceCube-Gen2与KM3NeT将大幅提升灵敏度，有望覆盖热退耦合轻暗物质参数空间。

Abstract: The IceCube and ANTARES collaborations have recently reported evidence for high-energy neutrinos associated with the Galactic plane and the Galactic Ridge, offering a new pathway to search for dark matter (DM). Deep inelastic scattering of cosmic rays with sub-GeV DM in the Galactic halo produces a distinctive neutrino signature from meson decays. Using detailed Galactic cosmic-ray maps and ANTARES observations, we derive 99% C.L. upper limits on the DM-nucleon cross section that extend down to keV-scale masses. These results establish Galactic neutrino telescopes as a powerful, complementary probe of light DM, with substantial improvements expected from upcoming IceCube-Gen2 and KM3NeT observations.

</details>
